Friday, October 16, 2026
coordinator11:25 PM

You are building the code-rendered music video for "2AM (And I'm Crying)", a club track sung from the point of view of a one-year-old baby. This is in the lineage of the "I'm Upping My P(doom)" videos made with Claude in Claude Code: every frame is a deterministic function of song time, painted in a browser, exported through headless Chrome and ffmpeg, with per-word karaoke typography.

Read these first (all in the repo /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop, a git repo):

  • songs/2am-crying/TREATMENT.md: the concept, style bible, and the plate-by-plate treatment. This is your brief for what to draw. Follow it; where it is silent, make choices in its spirit (deadpan, grid-locked, IR-greyscale nursery camera, the clock as the escalating HUD number).
  • songs/2am-crying/LYRICS.md and songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt: the lyrics and section structure.
  • README.md and .gitignore at the repo root.

Rules from the user's global config: never cd; run everything with absolute paths from the repo root; run slow commands (renders, installs) in the background and keep working; commit eagerly as normal commits whenever things reach a testable state (no amend/rebase/squash); end commit messages with the line Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>. Do not touch songs/2am-crying/synth/ (another agent owns it) or songs/baby-facts/ (another agent owns it). Do not commit audio or video files (the .gitignore handles *.wav *.mp3 *.mp4 and renders/ frames/).

Environment

  • macOS, Apple Silicon. Node 24.2, npm 11, ffmpeg 8.0 with libx264, Google Chrome at /Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome. uv and Python 3.14 exist if you need a small Python tool. No bun. Check whether npx playwright or puppeteer-core is the easier way to drive the installed Chrome; prefer puppeteer-core pointed at the system Chrome so nothing downloads a browser.
  • You can look at PNGs with the Read tool. Use that for visual iteration: render contact sheets, look at them, fix, repeat.

Architecture

  • shared/renderer/: the reusable engine, song-agnostic. A timeline (scene windows anchored to lyric lines, snapped to the beat grid), a karaoke band (per-word highlight, title-safe, reads lyrics.json), HUD helpers, easing/beat utilities (beatPos(t), pulse, seg, seeded hash(i)), a palette/theme mechanism, loaders for data/lyrics.json and data/audio.json, the preview page (open index.html?t=23 to scrub to a time, with play/pause against the audio file), and scripts/render.mjs (paint frames in headless Chrome, pipe PNGs or raw RGBA to ffmpeg, encode with libx264 CRF 16 + AAC from the song file; also --sheet contact sheets and --stills t1,t2,...). Frames must be renderable in parallel and out of order, so scenes carry no state between frames.
  • songs/2am-crying/video/: song-specific: palette, the baby character, props (crib, slats, mobile, Hatch light, phone notification card, monitor UI), one module per plate under scenes/, and timeline.js (the edit: which plate covers which lyric lines, with escalation params like hook n=1..4).
  • Canvas 2D is fine and probably best for this look (IR greyscale, heavy type, line art). Use three.js only if you really want it. Fonts: load from Google Fonts in the preview page but make the renderer wait for document.fonts.ready; Archivo Black or Inter 900 for slams, IBM Plex Mono for HUD/notifications, Nunito for the bridge.

Data

Two JSON inputs, same schemas as mexicat/pdoom-video so the alignment pipeline another agent is building drops in later:

data/lyrics.json:

{ "lines": [ { "i": int, "text": str, "start": float, "end": float,
               "words": [ { "w": str, "start": float, "end": float, "conf": float,
                            "syl": [[start,end],...]  // optional } ] } ],
  "extras": [ { "start": float, "end": float, "desc": str } ], "notes": str }

data/audio.json: { "bpm": float, "beats": [float], "downbeats": [float], "sections": [ { "name": str, "start": float, "end": float } ], "kicks": [float], "duration": float } (add fields if you need them; document them).

There is no vocal recording yet. Write songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs that generates both files from: bpm 128, a section plan (intro 4 bars, verse1 16, pre1 4, chorus1 8, post1 4, verse2 16, pre2 4, chorus2 8, bridge 16 at half-time feel but same bar length, final chorus 10, outro 4; adjust to taste but every boundary on a bar line), and the lyric sheet, distributing each section's lines across its bars and each line's words evenly across the line's span (leave the last 10 percent of each line silent). Spoken intro/outro lines get their own spans. If songs/2am-crying/synth/out/song.json exists when you run (another agent is rendering the synth demo and writes bpm, section start/end seconds, a beat grid and hook melody onsets there), prefer its sections and beat grid over the plan; print which source you used. Keep the mock tool so timings can be regenerated when the real alignment lands.

Audio for previews and the draft render: use songs/2am-crying/synth/out/*.wav if present (pick the full-song file, not the 10-second previews). If absent when you need it, generate a 128 BPM click + kick track with ffmpeg (aevalsrc/sine filters) of the planned length so the render pipeline is testable end to end, and note it in the README.

Deliverables, in order

  1. Engine + preview page working with the mock data and a placeholder scene that shows the HUD clock, the karaoke band, and beat flashes. Commit.
  2. scripts/render.mjs producing a 10-second 960x540 30 fps MP4 and a contact sheet. Commit.
  3. Plates, in this priority: hook (all four escalations, including the 11am hard cut to the day palette with the bar of near-silence), notif, meerkat, paci, nap, outro, bounce, then the rest. Every plate listed in TREATMENT.md must exist by the end; ones you don't have time to do properly get a deliberate minimal version (the HUD, the karaoke band, the IR nursery background and one simple motif) rather than an empty title card. Commit after every two or three plates.
  4. A full-length 960x540 30 fps draft render over the synth audio (or click track) at songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-540p.mp4 (gitignored), plus a contact sheet PNG of one frame per plate at songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/plates.png (commit this one; it is how the human reviews your work).
  5. songs/2am-crying/video/README.md: commands for preview, draft render, final 1080p60 render, regenerating mock timing, and a per-plate status table (done / minimal).

Work independently to completion. In your final report give: the plate status table, the paths of the draft MP4 and the plates contact sheet, the render time per frame at 540p, the exact commands, and any treatment points you deliberately changed and why.

1	# Treatment: 2AM (And I'm Crying)
2	
3	## Concept
4	
5	The whole video is the baby monitor. We watch the night through the nursery camera:
6	infrared greyscale, a timestamp in the corner, a baby who knows exactly what it is doing.
7	The clock is this song's "p(doom) number": it sits in the HUD all night and, on each
8	chorus line, it leaves its corner and slams full-screen to the next time (2:00, 3:00,
9	4:00, 5:40, 11:00). The club is not a metaphor the baby reaches for; it is the nursery
10	rendered honestly. Crib slats strobe like laser bars, the mobile is a disco ball, the
11	white-noise machine is a speaker stack, the Hatch night-light is the red strobe, and the
12	parents' bounce in the hallway is the kick drum.
13	
14	Tone: deadpan. The baby never mugs. Jokes land because the camera is calm and the
15	typography is confident. Funny like a safety form, not like a cartoon.
16	
17	## Style bible
18	
19	- **Palettes.** Night: near-black (#0b0b0e), IR greyscale (#c9ccd1 highlights, #5a5e66 mids),
20	  one red accent (#ff2a2a, the Hatch), a rare green (#39ff7a) for "LIVE" / monitor UI.
21	  Day (bridge only): inverted; cream paper (#f4efe6), warm grey ink (#3a3733), sunlight
22	  amber (#f5b14a) through blinds. The day/night inversion is the structural beat of the edit.
23	- **Type.** Hook slams: a heavy grotesque (Archivo Black or Inter 900). HUD, notifications,
24	  tabs, timers: a mono (IBM Plex Mono / JetBrains Mono). Bridge: a soft rounded sans
25	  (Nunito or Quicksand). Lyric karaoke band at the bottom, per-word highlight, title-safe.
26	- **Baby.** A procedurally drawn character: round head, two IR-glowing eyes, a sleep sack
27	  (a soft trapezoid), stubby arms. Eight to twelve strokes. Readable as a silhouette.
28	  Never cute-ified beyond that; the deadpan depends on it.
29	- **Motion.** Everything moves on the grid. Cuts on downbeats. Strobes and slams on kicks.
30	  The shush is the hi-hat: every offbeat gets a small "shh" particle. Strong eases,
31	  overshoot on slams. No handheld wobble except the parent's bounce.
32	- **HUD.** Top-left: `NURSERY CAM  •  02:00 AM`. Top-right: `● LIVE` in green during
33	  choruses. Bottom-left: a thin "sleep debt" bar that fills across the song and empties
34	  during the bridge. The HUD never overlaps the karaoke band.
35	- **Bans.** No stock-photo realism, no neon cyberpunk, no emoji-style faces, no actual
36	  photos of anyone.
37	
38	## Plates
39	
40	Each plate is anchored to lyric lines by content, never by hard-coded seconds. Windows
41	snap to the beat grid.
42	
43	| id | anchor | treatment |
44	|---|---|---|
45	| `notif` | Intro | Black. A phone notification card slides in: group-chat name, "Baby asleep. 7:45." The card's thumbnail zooms until it *is* the monitor feed; the HUD boots. The beat fades in with the zoom. |
46	| `routine` | V1 L1-2 | Three icons stamp on consecutive beats: tub, book, toothbrush (toothpaste squeezed on the beat). The Hatch cycles to red. The white noise is drawn as a grain field whose density tracks the hats. "one extremely slow shush": a single "shhhhhh" stretched across the bar, letters spacing out. |
47	| `vault` | V1 L3-4 | The nursery door drawn as a bank-vault wheel. A parent silhouette tiptoes out, foot by foot, on the beats. Cut to the baby's eyes opening in IR. An x-ray outline of the parent appears through the closed door with a label: *object permanence (Piaget, ~8 mo)*. |
48	| `venn` | V1 L5-6 | Two circles, DROWSY and AWAKE. The intersection label reads `n = 0`. On "awake" the AWAKE circle floods the frame. |
49	| `meerkat` | V1 L7-8 | Full IR monitor look: timestamp, battery, signal bars. The baby rises in the crib in one motion. Zoom into the glowing eyes. "ad-lib:" draws a small microphone icon next to the mouth. |
50	| `paci` | Pre-chorus | Side cross-section of the crib. The pacifier falls through the slats in slow motion on the first clause, lands under the crib. A dotted sightline from eye to paci: "I can see it." A reach arc that fails: "I can't reach it." A second arc from parent silhouette that succeeds: "You can reach it." Riser builds; "Here's how I reach you:" then hard cut. |
51	| `hook` (n=1..4) | each chorus | One word per hit, full-frame heavy type. On each timestamp the HUD clock rolls full-screen to the new time with a flip-clock motion, then returns to its corner. Slats strobe as laser bars on the kick; the mobile spins as a disco ball. Escalation by n: (1) plain IR; (2) red Hatch strobe added, "you're" in red; (3) red + green, frame shake, "we're" split across both parents' silhouettes; (4) sunrise amber bleeds in on "5:40", "good morning!" in yellow. The 11am line always does the same thing: hard cut to the day palette, baby asleep, one bar of near-silence, then the drop resumes. |
52	| `bounce` | Post-chorus | Hallway, long perspective. Parent silhouette bouncing the baby, exactly on the kick. A BPM readout counts up and locks at 128. Each "shh" spawns a hi-hat particle. |
53	| `tabs` | V2 L1-2 | A phone at 4am, brightness slider at 5%. Tabs multiply across the frame: each one's headline is "Is this normal?" and each answer is "Yes." A final search, "when does it end," returns an empty results page. |
54	| `theories` | V2 L3-4 | "teeth?" a gum diagram counts to eight. "Gas?" a burp log with one entry: 21:00. "A leap?" a horoscope wheel whose signs are Wonder Weeks "leap" numbers; a paywall modal pops over it. |
55	| `ferber` | V2 L5-6 | Three timers labeled 3:00, 5:00, 10:00. The parent's timer bails at 1:00. Then the chair method: a chair on floor tiles, one tile further each night; the parent sits, the baby stands; two glowing eyes vs two tired eyes; "Neither of us won." |
56	| `stem` | V2 L7-8 | The bounce as a sine wave over the hallway; the wave's period snaps to 128 BPM. Then a DAW track list: KICK, BASS, PAD, HATS, and a track named BABY whose waveform is the vocal. |
57	| `volume` | Pre-chorus 2 | Nightstand, monitor, volume bar sliding to max. The baby's waveform fills the little screen. "You hear everything." |
58	| `nap` | Bridge | Inversion to the day palette. Sunlight through blinds, slow. The baby asleep. A filing cabinet labeled HIPPOCAMPUS files cards: "stairs," "dog," "your face at 4." A twitch: a small spark on a hand, and a dotted line to a body map lighting the matching region (*sensorimotor mapping*). Notification card: "Baby asleep, 10:45." Cut to a sink of dishes; the parent's hands; the club beat is a tiny pulsing dot heard through the wall. "I will be perfect." The riser returns the night palette. |
59	| `outro` | Outro | Notification card: "Baby awake. 11:52." Black. One single-frame white strobe on the last "untz." |
60	
61	## Technical notes
62	
63	- Deterministic: every frame is a pure function of song time. No `Math.random()`; use a
64	  seeded hash per object.
65	- Inputs: `data/lyrics.json` (word-level timings, the schema from mexicat/pdoom-video:
66	  lines → words → {w, start, end, conf, syl}) and `data/audio.json` (bpm, beats,
67	  downbeats, sections, kick onsets). Until the Suno vocal exists, both are mocked from
68	  the synth demo's `song.json` and the lyric sheet.
69	- Output: 1920x1080 at 60 fps for finals; 960x540 at 30 fps for drafts and contact sheets.
70	- Headless Chrome paints frames; ffmpeg encodes (libx264, CRF 16, AAC).
71

1	# 2AM (And I'm Crying)
2	
3	Untz-untz club track, sung from the point of view of a one-year-old. The chorus is a
4	transcription of what the baby's mother said; the verses are the baby's deadpan account
5	of the night, packed with parent-internet jargon. 128 BPM, A minor, four on the floor.
6	
7	Escalation device (the "p(doom) number" of this song): the clock. Each chorus reads the
8	same three timestamps, but the third line's pronoun moves one notch: *I'm* crying →
9	*still* crying → *you're* crying → *we're* crying → *good morning*. The 11am line is
10	always the punchline and always lands on a beat drop.
11	
12	Bracketed notes like `[brand]` mark things to swap for whatever's actually in the nursery.
13	
14	---
15	
16	## Canonical lyrics (v1)
17	
18	**[Intro]** *(spoken, flat, a group-chat notification read aloud; white noise; the beat fades in)*
19	> Baby asleep. Seven forty-five.
20	> *(untz. untz. untz. untz.)*
21	
22	**[Verse 1]**
23	> Seven-thirty: bath, then book, then toothpaste on the brush,
24	> Hatch to red, white noise to "shower," one extremely slow shush.
25	> You tiptoe out the door like you're cracking a vault.
26	> I've got object permanence now. That's Piaget's fault.
27	> "Drowsy but awake," they say. A myth. A hoax. A fake.
28	> No one's seen it in the wild. I'm choosing one: awake.
29	> Night-vision on the Nanit, little meerkat in the crib,
30	> Greyscale eyes in close-up. Here's my 2am ad-lib:
31	
32	**[Pre-Chorus]** *(beat thins to kick + riser; each clause a half-bar)*
33	> I dropped the paci through the slats. It's under the crib. I can see it.
34	> I can't reach it. You can reach it. Here's how I reach you:
35	
36	**[Chorus]**
37	> It's 2am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
38	> It's 3am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
39	> It's 4am and I'm... still crying
40	> It's 11am and I'm napping
41	
42	**[Post-Chorus]** *(the shush as hi-hat)*
43	> *(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)*
44	> *(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)*
45	
46	**[Verse 2]**
47	> Four a.m., you Google "is this normal" with the brightness low.
48	> Every tab says "normal." None of them say when it'll go.
49	> You whisper "teeth?" I've got eight and they're fine.
50	> "Gas?" I burped at nine. "A leap?" That's a horoscope with a timeline.
51	> Ferber says to check at three, then five, then ten. You lasted one.
52	> The chair method: you sat. I stood. We stared. Neither of us won.
53	> You bounce me down the hallway, hundred-twenty-eight BPM.
54	> That's where I got the beat. The untz is you. I'm just the stem.
55	
56	**[Pre-Chorus]**
57	> The monitor's on your nightstand. Volume: max. You hear everything.
58	> You hear everything. Here's how I reach you:
59	
60	**[Chorus]**
61	> It's 2am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
62	> It's 3am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
63	> It's 4am and you're crying
64	> It's 11am and I'm napping
65	
66	**[Bridge]** *(half-time; kick out; music box / celesta plays the hook; the club beat continues muffled, as if through a wall)*
67	> It's 11am and I'm napping.
68	> Hippocampus humming. I'm consolidating:
69	> the stairs, the word for "dog," your face at four.
70	> If I twitch, that's my brain, learning where my hands are.
71	> The group chat gets its post: "Baby asleep, 10:45."
72	> And you? You could sleep. You're doing dishes.
73	> "Sleep when the baby sleeps." You chose dishes.
74	> I'll be up at twelve-ten. I will be perfect.
75	> And tonight we do it all again:
76	
77	**[Final Chorus]** *(full drop, top octave, maybe the C-major lift)*
78	> It's 2am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
79	> It's 3am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
80	> It's 4am and we're crying
81	> It's 5:40 and... good morning!
82	> It's 11am and I'm napping
83	
84	**[Outro]** *(beat cuts to white noise; spoken, flat)*
85	> Baby awake. Eleven fifty-two.
86	> *(untz.)*
87

90	## Reference key (what each line is doing)
91	
92	| Line | Reference |
93	|---|---|
94	| bath, book, toothpaste | the "bath, book, bed" routine; brushing with toothpaste is a house rule |
95	| Hatch to red / "shower" | Hatch sound machine, red = sleep; white noise "as loud as a shower" (Karp) |
96	| slow shush | the "S" of the 5 S's (Happiest Baby on the Block) |
97	| object permanence / Piaget | emerges ~8 months; the baby knows you're behind the door |
98	| drowsy but awake | the most-repeated and least-observed piece of sleep advice |
99	| Nanit / meerkat | infrared baby-cam; the standing-baby-staring-at-camera meme |
100	| paci through the slats | pacifier re-insertion, the canonical 2am wake |
101	| "is this normal" | yes; the internet never says until when |
102	| eight teeth | typical count around 12-13 months (verify against your kid) |
103	| horoscope with a timeline | The Wonder Weeks "leaps" have not replicated |
104	| three, five, ten | Ferber's graduated check-in intervals, night 1 |
105	| chair method | sit in a chair, move it further out each night |
106	| 128 BPM | parents bounce at roughly dance tempo; the song's tempo is the bounce |
107	| stem | audio stem; the baby is the vocal stem of the household mix |
108	| consolidating | naps consolidate memory (Spencer, UMass; Netflix *Babies* "Sleep") |
109	| twitch / where my hands are | sleep twitches map the sensorimotor system (Blumberg, Iowa) |
110	| "Baby asleep, 10:45" | the family group chat ritual |
111	| sleep when the baby sleeps / dishes | the advice nobody can take |
112	| 5:40 good morning | early morning waking; the cheerful dawn baby |
113	
114	## Variants to try in Suno
115	
116	- **v1-hook-only**: strip verses to the chorus + post-chorus + bridge, for a 90-second cut.
117	- **v1-named**: swap "little meerkat" for the kid's name in the Nanit line, and let the
118	  outro notification read the real group-chat format.
119	- **v1-house-verse** (optional 3rd verse, if the song needs more length): grandparents on
120	  the fourth floor cooking lunch, the rice cooker, My Gym at ten, the nanny shift change,
121	  "two naps a day, ten-thirty and three." Not written yet; only if Suno runs short.
122	
123	## Open lyric questions for Jalex
124	
125	1. "you're crying" in chorus 2: keep? It is the emotional turn of the song, but it puts a
126	   parent crying on record.
127	2. Brand names (Hatch, Nanit): keep as in-jokes, swap for your actual gear, or genericize
128	   ("the red light," "the camera")?
129	3. "Eight teeth": change to the real count.

# Suno run pack: 2AM (And I'm Crying)

Custom mode (lyrics supplied). Model v5.5. Each `suno generate` call returns 2 clips and
costs about 70 credits on v5.5 (v4.5-all is the cheap option for exploratory runs).

Lyrics file for the CLI: `suno_lyrics_v1.txt` (section markers only, no prose notes).

## Run A: deadpan Eurodance (primary)

```
suno generate \
  --title "2AM (And I'm Crying)" \
  --model v5.5 \
  --vocal female \
  --tags "eurodance, big room house, 128 bpm, four-on-the-floor, sidechained supersaw pads, offbeat open hi-hats, clap on 2 and 4, pitched-down untz vocal chop, deadpan female pop vocal, clear diction, bright hyperpop sheen, euphoric drop on the chorus, half-time music box bridge, white noise riser, spoken intro and outro, club anthem, 2009 dance pop" \
  --exclude "acoustic, ballad, slow tempo, country, rock guitar, screamo, metal, lo-fi, mumbled vocals, vocal runs, melisma" \
  --style-influence 70 --weirdness 35 \
  --lyrics-file /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt \
  --wait --download /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/audio/runA
```

## Run B: chipmunk hyperpop (the baby actually sings it)

Same lyrics. Tags swap the vocal: "pitched-up childlike female vocal, hyperpop, chipmunk
formant, glitchy vocal chops, 128 bpm eurodance". Weirdness 55.

```
suno generate \
  --title "2AM (And I'm Crying) [hyper]" \
  --model v5.5 \
  --vocal female \
  --tags "hyperpop eurodance, 128 bpm, four-on-the-floor, pitched-up childlike vocal, formant-shifted lead vocal, glitchy vocal chops, sidechained supersaw, offbeat hats, euphoric drop, half-time music box bridge, spoken intro and outro" \
  --exclude "acoustic, ballad, slow tempo, country, rock guitar, metal, lo-fi, mumbled vocals" \
  --style-influence 60 --weirdness 55 \
  --lyrics-file /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt \
  --wait --download /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/audio/runB
```

## Run C: the absurd one (bass-baritone baby)

Same lyrics, `--vocal male`, tags "deep deadpan baritone, eurodance, 128 bpm, ...". One run
only; it's a gag, but a gag that might win.

## What to listen for when picking

- The chorus pronoun shift must be audible: I'm / still / you're / we're / good morning.
- The 11am line should land after a drop or a stop; if Suno buries it, add `[Drop]` or a
  blank line before it in the lyrics file.
- The bridge must actually go half-time and quiet. If it doesn't, move the `[Bridge]` tag
  to `[Bridge - half-time, music box, soft]`.
- The spoken intro/outro should be spoken, not sung. If sung, prefix with `[Spoken]`.

## After a keeper

1. `suno timed-lyrics <clip_id> --lrc > lyrics.lrc` for a coarse line-level sync.
2. Run the `shared/align` pipeline for word-level timings (Demucs + CTC forced alignment,
   cross-checked with mlx-whisper), then the renderer reads `data/lyrics.json`.

"""Timeline: the beat grid, section layout, and expansion of the declarative
song spec into timed events. The renderer and song.json both read the events
produced here, so audio and metadata share one source of truth.

Times are exact multiples of a 16th (15/128 s at 128 BPM, exactly
representable in float64); sample positions are always round(t * sr).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from dsp import note_to_midi, midi_to_hz

VEL = {"X": 1.0, "x": 0.8, "o": 0.45, ".": 0.0, "-": 0.0}


# --------------------------------------------------------------- helpers ----
def per_bar(n: int, default: str | None = None, **overrides: str | None) -> list[str | None]:
    """A list of n per-bar patterns. Overrides use 1-based bar keys such as
    b5='...' or b5_8='...' (bars 5 through 8 inclusive)."""
    out = [default] * n
    for k, v in overrides.items():
        rng = k[1:].split("_")
        a = int(rng[0])
        b = int(rng[1]) if len(rng) > 1 else a
        for i in range(a, b + 1):
            out[i - 1] = v
    return out


def chords_every(names: list[str], bars_each: int, total: int) -> list[str]:
    seq = [c for c in names for _ in range(bars_each)]
    return [seq[i % len(seq)] for i in range(total)]


CHORD_QUALITY = {"": (0, 4, 7), "m": (0, 3, 7), "sus4": (0, 5, 7), "sus2": (0, 2, 7)}


def chord_pcs(name: str) -> tuple[int, tuple[int, ...]]:
    root = name[0]
    rest = name[1:]
    acc = 0
    if rest[:1] in ("#", "b"):
        acc = 1 if rest[0] == "#" else -1
        rest = rest[1:]
    pc = (note_to_midi(root + "4") - 60 + acc) % 12
    return pc, tuple((pc + iv) % 12 for iv in CHORD_QUALITY[rest])


def chord_notes(name: str, lo: int, hi: int) -> list[int]:
    """All chord tones with lo <= midi < hi."""
    _, pcs = chord_pcs(name)
    return [m for m in range(lo, hi) if m % 12 in pcs]


def bass_root(name: str, lo: int = 40) -> int:
    pc, _ = chord_pcs(name)
    return lo + ((pc - lo) % 12)


# ------------------------------------------------------------ data model ----
@dataclass
class Section:
    name: str
    bars: int
    chords: list[str]
    label: str = ""
    drums: dict[str, list[str | None]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    drum_gain: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)   # inst -> gain or (start, end) ramp
    bass: list[str | None] | None = None          # step patterns, root of current chord
    sub: bool = False                              # sustained sub under each chord
    pad: dict | None = None                        # gain, cutoff points, rhythm (stabs)
    arp: dict | None = None                        # voice, pattern(s), gain, register
    melody: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    fx: list[tuple] = field(default_factory=list)
    cut_last_beats: float = 0.0                    # tonal layers stop this many beats early

    def __post_init__(self):
        assert len(self.chords) == self.bars, (self.name, len(self.chords), self.bars)
        for k, v in self.drums.items():
            assert len(v) == self.bars, (self.name, k, len(v))


@dataclass
class Score:
    bpm: float
    sr: int
    sections: list[dict]
    total_bars: int
    music_end_s: float
    drum_hits: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]]
    chords: list[dict]
    bass: list[dict]
    subs: list[dict]
    pad: list[dict]
    pad_cutoff: list[tuple[float, float]]
    pad_gain: list[tuple[float, float]]
    arps: list[dict]
    melody: list[dict]
    fx: list[dict]


class Grid:
    def __init__(self, bpm: float, sr: int, beats_per_bar: int = 4):
        self.bpm, self.sr, self.bpb = bpm, sr, beats_per_bar
        self.beat = 60.0 / bpm
        self.bar = self.beat * beats_per_bar
        self.step = self.beat / 4  # 16th

    def t(self, bar: float, step16: float = 0.0) -> float:
        return bar * self.bar + step16 * self.step

    def sample(self, t: float) -> int:
        return int(round(t * self.sr))


# ------------------------------------------------------------- expansion ----
def _ramp(v, frac: float) -> float:
    if isinstance(v, tuple):
        return v[0] + (v[1] - v[0]) * frac
    return float(v)


def expand(sections: list[Section], bpm: float, sr: int, syllables: dict) -> Score:
    g = Grid(bpm, sr)
    sec_rows, chords, bass, subs, pad, arps, melody, fx = [], [], [], [], [], [], [], []
    drum_hits: dict[str, list[tuple[float, float]]] = {}
    cutoff_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    gain_pts: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    bar0 = 0
    for si, s in enumerate(sections):
        t0, t1 = g.t(bar0), g.t(bar0 + s.bars)
        tone_end = t1 - s.cut_last_beats * g.beat
        sec_rows.append(dict(name=s.name, label=s.label or s.name, start_bar=bar0, bars=s.bars,
                             start_s=t0, end_s=t1, start_sample=g.sample(t0), end_sample=g.sample(t1),
                             chords=list(s.chords)))
        # ---- drums: a pattern of N chars divides its bar into N equal steps
        for inst, pats in s.drums.items():
            for bi, p in enumerate(pats):
                if not p:
                    continue
                nst = len(p)
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    v = VEL.get(ch, 0.0)
                    if v <= 0:
                        continue
                    t = g.t(bar0 + bi) + k * g.bar / nst
                    frac = (t - t0) / (t1 - t0)
                    v *= _ramp(s.drum_gain.get(inst, 1.0), frac)
                    drum_hits.setdefault(inst, []).append((t, v))
        # ---- chords (merge repeats into held segments)
        bi = 0
        while bi < s.bars:
            bj = bi
            while bj + 1 < s.bars and s.chords[bj + 1] == s.chords[bi]:
                bj += 1
            ct0, ct1 = g.t(bar0 + bi), min(g.t(bar0 + bj + 1), tone_end)
            chords.append(dict(chord=s.chords[bi], start_s=ct0, end_s=g.t(bar0 + bj + 1), section=s.name))
            if s.sub:
                subs.append(dict(t=ct0, dur=ct1 - ct0, midi=bass_root(s.chords[bi]), section=s.name))
            if s.pad and not s.pad.get("rhythm"):
                pad.append(dict(t=ct0, dur=ct1 - ct0, notes=chord_notes(s.chords[bi], *s.pad.get("range", (55, 72))),
                                vel=1.0, section=s.name))
            bi = bj + 1
        # ---- pad stabs
        if s.pad and s.pad.get("rhythm"):
            gate = s.pad.get("gate_steps", 2)
            for b, p in enumerate(per_bar_list(s.pad["rhythm"], s.bars)):
                if not p:
                    continue
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    v = VEL.get(ch, 0)
                    t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / len(p)
                    if v > 0 and t < tone_end:
                        pad.append(dict(t=t, dur=min(gate * g.step, tone_end - t),
                                        notes=chord_notes(s.chords[b], *s.pad.get("range", (55, 72))),
                                        vel=v, section=s.name))
        if s.pad:
            for bar, hz in s.pad.get("cutoff", [(0, 8000.0)]):
                cutoff_pts.append((g.t(bar0 + bar), float(hz)))
            gi = s.pad.get("gain", 1.0)
            gi = gi if isinstance(gi, tuple) else (gi, gi)
            gain_pts += [(t0, gi[0]), (t1 - 1e-6, gi[1])]
        else:
            gain_pts += [(t0, 0.0), (t1 - 1e-6, 0.0)]
        # ---- bass: step patterns playing the current chord root
        if s.bass:
            for b, p in enumerate(s.bass):
                if not p:
                    continue
                nst = len(p)
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    v = VEL.get(ch, 0)
                    t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / nst
                    if v > 0 and t < tone_end:
                        bass.append(dict(t=t, dur=min(0.85 * g.bar / nst * (2 if nst <= 8 else 1), tone_end - t),
                                         midi=bass_root(s.chords[b]), vel=v, section=s.name))
        # ---- arpeggios: digits index the chord tones inside `range`
        if s.arp:
            pats = per_bar_list(s.arp["pattern"], s.bars)
            gain = s.arp.get("gain", 1.0)
            for b, p in enumerate(pats):
                if not p:
                    continue
                tones = chord_notes(s.chords[b], *s.arp.get("range", (64, 81)))
                for k, ch in enumerate(p):
                    if not ch.isdigit():
                        continue
                    t = g.t(bar0 + b) + k * g.bar / len(p)
                    if t >= tone_end:
                        continue
                    frac = (t - t0) / (t1 - t0)
                    arps.append(dict(t=t, midi=tones[int(ch) % len(tones)], vel=_ramp(gain, frac),
                                     voice=s.arp.get("voice", "pluck"), section=s.name))
        # ---- melody placements: lines of (step16, dur16, note, syllable)
        for m in s.melody:
            st = m.get("stretch", 1)
            for li, (line, at_bar) in enumerate(zip(m["lines"], m["bars"])):
                for (step, dur, note, syl) in line["notes"]:
                    t = g.t(bar0 + at_bar, step * st)
                    key = syl.lower()
                    info = syllables.get(key, {})
                    midi = note_to_midi(note) + m.get("transpose", 0)
                    melody.append(dict(
                        onset_s=t, duration_s=dur * st * g.step, midi=midi,
                        freq_hz=float(midi_to_hz(midi)), note=note, syllable=syl,
                        vel=(1.0 if syl.isupper() else 0.82) * m.get("gain", 1.0),
                        line=line["line"], lyric=line["lyric"], voice=m["voice"], section=s.name,
                        vowels=info.get("v", ("a", "E")), onset=info.get("onset"),
                        coda=info.get("coda"), cry=bool(info.get("cry")) and m["voice"] == "cry",
                    ))
        # ---- fx: (kind, bar_offset, length_in_bars[, gain])
        for f in s.fx:
            kind, at, ln = f[0], f[1], f[2]
            gain = f[3] if len(f) > 3 else 1.0
            fx.append(dict(kind=kind, t=g.t(bar0 + at), dur=ln * g.bar, gain=gain, section=s.name))
        bar0 += s.bars
    melody.sort(key=lambda e: (e["voice"], e["onset_s"]))
    for k in drum_hits:
        drum_hits[k].sort()
    return Score(bpm=bpm, sr=sr, sections=sec_rows, total_bars=bar0, music_end_s=g.t(bar0),
                 drum_hits=drum_hits, chords=chords, bass=bass, subs=subs, pad=pad,
                 pad_cutoff=cutoff_pts, pad_gain=gain_pts, arps=arps, melody=melody, fx=fx)


def per_bar_list(p, bars: int) -> list[str | None]:
    if isinstance(p, str) or p is None:
        return [p] * bars
    assert len(p) == bars
    return list(p)

/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/synth/timeline.py:2:song spec into timed events. The renderer and song.json both read the events
BashLook for synth section names11:32 PM

file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Mock timing generator for "2AM (And I'm Crying)".
//
// Writes data/lyrics.json and data/audio.json (mexicat/pdoom-video schemas) from
//   - the lyric sheet  songs/2am-crying/suno_lyrics_v1.txt
//   - a section plan    (PLAN below: 128 BPM, every boundary on a bar line)
//   - or, when present, songs/2am-crying/synth/out/song.json (sections + beat grid
//     from the synth demo), which wins over the plan.
//
// Each section's lines are laid out across its bars (LINE_BARS, default even), each
// line's words evenly across the line's span, leaving the last 10% of the span silent.
// Beat-locked lines (untz / shh) put one word per beat or offbeat instead.
//
// Usage:  node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs [--plan] [--out <dir>]
//   --plan   ignore synth/out/song.json even if it exists
//
// Keep this tool: re-run it whenever the plan or the synth grid changes, and replace its
// lyrics.json with the real alignment (shared/align) once the vocal exists.

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const VIDEO = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
const SONG = path.resolve(VIDEO, '..');
const LYRICS_TXT = path.join(SONG, 'suno_lyrics_v1.txt');
const SYNTH_JSON = path.join(SONG, 'synth', 'out', 'song.json');

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const forcePlan = args.includes('--plan');
const outDir = args.includes('--out') ? path.resolve(args[args.indexOf('--out') + 1]) : path.join(VIDEO, 'data');

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ the plan ----
const BPM = 128;
const BEATS_PER_BAR = 4;
// name, bars, kick (which bars carry the four-on-the-floor), hats (offbeat hats)
const PLAN = [
  { name: 'intro',   bars: 4,  kick: 'from3', hats: false },
  { name: 'verse1',  bars: 16, kick: 'all',   hats: true },
  { name: 'pre1',    bars: 4,  kick: 'all',   hats: false, riser: true },
  { name: 'chorus1', bars: 8,  kick: 'all',   hats: true },
  { name: 'post1',   bars: 4,  kick: 'all',   hats: true },
  { name: 'verse2',  bars: 16, kick: 'all',   hats: true },
  { name: 'pre2',    bars: 4,  kick: 'all',   hats: false, riser: true },
  { name: 'chorus2', bars: 8,  kick: 'all',   hats: true },
  { name: 'bridge',  bars: 16, kick: 'none',  hats: false, riserLastBars: 2 },
  { name: 'final',   bars: 10, kick: 'all',   hats: true },
  { name: 'outro',   bars: 4,  kick: 'last',  hats: false },
];
// Bars per lyric line (must sum to the section's bars). Omitted = even split.
const LINE_BARS = {
  intro: [2, 2],
  bridge: [2, 1.5, 2.5, 2, 2, 1.5, 1.5, 2, 1],
};
// Fraction of a line's span that carries words (default 0.9). The 11am line is sung in
// its first bar; its second bar is the near-silence before the drop resumes.
const FILL_DEFAULT = 0.9;
const fillFor = (section, text) => (/^it'?s 11am/i.test(text) && /chorus|final/.test(section) ? 0.45 : FILL_DEFAULT);

// Section headers in the lyric sheet, in order, map onto plan names in order.
const HEADER_KINDS = ['intro', 'verse', 'pre', 'chorus', 'post', 'verse', 'pre', 'chorus', 'bridge', 'final', 'outro'];

// ------------------------------------------------------------ lyric sheet ----
function parseLyricSheet(txt) {
  const sections = [];
  for (const raw of txt.split(/\r?\n/)) {
    const line = raw.trim();
    if (!line) continue;
    const m = line.match(/^\[(.+)\]$/);
    if (m) { sections.push({ header: m[1], lines: [] }); continue; }
    if (!sections.length) throw new Error('lyric line before any [Section] header: ' + line);
    sections.at(-1).lines.push(line);
  }
  if (sections.length !== PLAN.length) {
    throw new Error(`lyric sheet has ${sections.length} sections, plan has ${PLAN.length}`);
  }
  return sections.map((s, i) => ({ ...s, name: PLAN[i].name }));
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------ synth reader ----
function kindOf(name) {
  const n = String(name).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  if (/^intro/.test(n)) return 'intro';
  if (/^outro/.test(n)) return 'outro';
  if (/^bridge/.test(n)) return 'bridge';
  if (/^(final|last)|chorus3|finalchorus/.test(n)) return 'final';
  if (/^post/.test(n)) return 'post';
  if (/^pre/.test(n)) return 'pre';
  if (/^(chorus|hook|drop)/.test(n)) return 'chorus';
  if (/^verse|^v\d/.test(n)) return 'verse';
  return 'other';
}

function readSynth() {
  if (forcePlan || !fs.existsSync(SYNTH_JSON)) return null;
  let j;
  try { j = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(SYNTH_JSON, 'utf8')); } catch (e) {
    console.warn(`[mock_timing] could not parse ${SYNTH_JSON}: ${e.message}; using plan`);
    return null;
  }
  const bpm = Number(j.bpm) || BPM;
  const rows = (j.sections || []).map((s) => ({
    name: s.name ?? s.label,
    start: Number(s.start_s ?? s.start),
    end: Number(s.end_s ?? s.end),
  })).filter((s) => Number.isFinite(s.start) && Number.isFinite(s.end));
  // Assign plan names by order of kind.
  const counters = {};
  const wanted = { verse: ['verse1', 'verse2'], pre: ['pre1', 'pre2'], chorus: ['chorus1', 'chorus2', 'final'],
    post: ['post1'], intro: ['intro'], outro: ['outro'], bridge: ['bridge'], final: ['final'] };
  const mapped = {};
  for (const r of rows) {
    const k = kindOf(r.name);
    if (k === 'other') continue;
    const idx = counters[k] = (counters[k] ?? -1) + 1;
    const nm = wanted[k]?.[idx];
    if (!nm) continue;
    if (mapped[nm]) { mapped[nm].end = Math.max(mapped[nm].end, r.end); continue; }
    mapped[nm] = { name: nm, start: r.start, end: r.end, src: r.name };
  }
  const missing = PLAN.map((p) => p.name).filter((n) => !mapped[n]);
  if (missing.length) {
    console.warn(`[mock_timing] synth song.json lacks sections ${missing.join(', ')}; using plan`);
    return null;
  }
  let beats = Array.isArray(j.beats) ? j.beats.map(Number)
    : Array.isArray(j.beat_grid) ? j.beat_grid.map(Number)
    : Array.isArray(j.grid?.beats) ? j.grid.beats.map(Number) : null;
  const end = Number(j.duration ?? j.music_end_s ?? j.duration_s ?? Math.max(...rows.map((r) => r.end)));
  if (!beats) {
    const bd = 60 / bpm;
    beats = [];
    for (let k = 0; k * bd < end - 1e-6; k++) beats.push(+(k * bd).toFixed(6));
  }
  const kicksRaw = j.kicks ?? j.drum_hits?.kick ?? j.drums?.kick ?? null;
  const kicks = Array.isArray(kicksRaw) ? kicksRaw.map((k) => Number(Array.isArray(k) ? k[0] : (k.t ?? k))) : null;
  const melody = Array.isArray(j.melody) ? j.melody : (Array.isArray(j.hook_onsets) ? j.hook_onsets : null);
  return { bpm, sections: PLAN.map((p) => mapped[p.name]), beats, kicks, duration: end, melody, raw: j };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ build ----
const r6 = (x) => Math.round(x * 1e6) / 1e6;

function build() {
  const sheet = parseLyricSheet(fs.readFileSync(LYRICS_TXT, 'utf8'));
  const synth = readSynth();
  const source = synth ? `synth (${path.relative(path.resolve(SONG, '..', '..'), SYNTH_JSON)})` : 'plan';
  const bpm = synth ? synth.bpm : BPM;
  const beatDur = 60 / bpm;
  const barDur = beatDur * BEATS_PER_BAR;

  // Beat grid. Plan: start at 0. Synth: as given.
  let beats, sections;
  if (synth) {
    beats = synth.beats;
    sections = synth.sections.map((s) => ({ name: s.name, start: r6(s.start), end: r6(s.end) }));
  } else {
    const totalBars = PLAN.reduce((a, p) => a + p.bars, 0);
    beats = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < totalBars * BEATS_PER_BAR; k++) beats.push(r6(k * beatDur));
    sections = [];
    let bar = 0;
    for (const p of PLAN) {
      sections.push({ name: p.name, start: r6(bar * barDur), end: r6((bar + p.bars) * barDur) });
      bar += p.bars;
    }
  }
  const duration = synth ? r6(synth.duration) : sections.at(-1).end;
  // Beat-index helpers so line placement follows the grid even if tempo drifts.
  const beatIndexNear = (t) => {
    let best = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < beats.length; i++) if (Math.abs(beats[i] - t) < Math.abs(beats[best] - t)) best = i;
    return best;
  };
  const beatTime = (fi) => {
    // fractional beat index -> seconds, extrapolating past the grid
    const i = Math.floor(fi), f = fi - i;
    const at = (k) => (k < beats.length ? beats[k] : beats.at(-1) + (k - beats.length + 1) * beatDur);
    return at(i) + f * (at(i + 1) - at(i));
  };
  const downbeats = beats.filter((_, i) => i % BEATS_PER_BAR === 0);

  const lines = [];
  const extras = [];
  const kicks = [];
  const hats = [];
  const quiet = [];
  const risers = [];

  sections.forEach((sec, si) => {
    const plan = PLAN[si];
    const sheetSec = sheet[si];
    const b0 = beatIndexNear(sec.start);
    const secBeats = Math.max(1, beatIndexNear(sec.end) - b0);
    const secBars = secBeats / BEATS_PER_BAR;
    let lb = LINE_BARS[sec.name] ?? sheetSec.lines.map(() => plan.bars / sheetSec.lines.length);
    if (lb.length !== sheetSec.lines.length) throw new Error(`LINE_BARS.${sec.name} has ${lb.length} entries for ${sheetSec.lines.length} lines`);
    const scale = secBars / lb.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0); // synth sections may differ in length
    let cursor = b0; // beat index
    const lineSpans = [];
    sheetSec.lines.forEach((text, li) => {
      const nb = Math.max(1, Math.round(lb[li] * scale * BEATS_PER_BAR * 2) / 2); // half-beat snap
      lineSpans.push({ text, b: cursor, nb });
      cursor += nb;
    });

    for (const { text, b, nb } of lineSpans) {
      const tokens = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
      const t0 = beatTime(b), t1 = beatTime(b + nb);
      let words;
      const isUntz = /^\(?untz/i.test(tokens[0] ?? '');
      if (isUntz) {
        // Beat-locked: untz on beats; shh on the offbeats of the following bar.
        const nUntz = tokens.filter((w) => /untz/i.test(w)).length;
        const untzStep = sec.name === 'intro' ? 2 : 1; // intro: half notes, slow and spoken
        const untzLead = sec.name === 'outro' ? nb - BEATS_PER_BAR : (sec.name === 'intro' ? 0 : 0);
        let ui = 0, si2 = 0;
        words = tokens.map((w) => {
          if (/untz/i.test(w)) {
            const bi = b + untzLead + ui++ * untzStep;
            return { w, start: beatTime(bi), end: beatTime(bi + 0.8) };
          }
          const bi = b + nUntz * untzStep + si2++ + 0.5; // offbeats after the untz run
          return { w, start: beatTime(bi), end: beatTime(bi + 0.4) };
        });
      } else {
        const fill = fillFor(sec.name, text);
        const span = (t1 - t0) * fill;
        const n = tokens.length;
        words = tokens.map((w, k) => ({ w, start: t0 + (k * span) / n, end: t0 + ((k + 1) * span) / n }));
      }
      words = words.map((w) => ({ w: w.w, start: r6(w.start), end: r6(w.end), conf: 1.0 }));
      lines.push({ i: lines.length, text, start: words[0].start, end: words.at(-1).end, section: sec.name, words });

      if (/^it'?s 11am/i.test(text) && /chorus|final/.test(sec.name)) {
        quiet.push({ start: r6(t0), end: r6(t1), why: '11am line + near-silent bar' });
        extras.push({ start: r6(words.at(-1).end), end: r6(t1), desc: 'near-silence: one bar before the drop resumes' });
      }
    }

    // Kicks / hats for this section (plan semantics; synth kicks override below).
    for (let k = 0; k < secBeats; k++) {
      const bar = Math.floor(k / BEATS_PER_BAR);
      const t = beatTime(b0 + k);
      const inQuiet = quiet.some((q) => t >= q.start - 1e-6 && t < q.end - 1e-6);
      let kick = false;
      if (plan.kick === 'all') kick = true;
      else if (plan.kick === 'from3') kick = bar >= 2;
      else if (plan.kick === 'last') kick = k === secBeats - BEATS_PER_BAR; // the single last untz
      if (kick && !inQuiet) kicks.push(r6(t));
      if (plan.hats && !inQuiet) hats.push(r6(beatTime(b0 + k + 0.5)));
    }
    if (plan.riser) risers.push({ start: sec.start, end: sec.end });
    if (plan.riserLastBars) risers.push({ start: r6(beatTime(b0 + secBeats - plan.riserLastBars * BEATS_PER_BAR)), end: sec.end });
    if (sec.name === 'intro') extras.push({ start: sec.start, end: sec.end, desc: 'white noise; the beat fades in' });
    if (sec.name === 'outro') extras.push({ start: sec.start, end: sec.end, desc: 'beat cuts to white noise' });
    if (sec.name === 'post1') extras.push({ start: sec.start, end: sec.end, desc: 'the shush as hi-hat' });
    if (sec.name === 'bridge') extras.push({ start: sec.start, end: sec.end, desc: 'half-time, music box, club beat muffled through the wall' });
  });

  const kicksOut = synth?.kicks?.length ? synth.kicks.map(r6) : kicks;

  const lyrics = {
    lines,
    extras: extras.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start),
    notes: `MOCK timing (tools/mock_timing.mjs, source: ${source}). Words are spread evenly across each line; ` +
      `untz/shh are beat-locked. Replace with the shared/align output once the vocal exists.`,
  };
  const audio = {
    bpm,
    beats,
    downbeats,
    sections,
    kicks: kicksOut,
    duration,
    // ---- added fields (optional; the renderer works without them) ----
    beats_per_bar: BEATS_PER_BAR,
    hats,                       // offbeat hi-hat onsets (seconds)
    quiet,                      // spans where the mix is near-silent (the 11am bar)
    risers,                     // spans with a riser building
    source,                     // 'plan' or 'synth (...)'
  };
  if (synth?.melody) audio.hook_onsets = synth.melody.map((m) => r6(Number(m.onset_s ?? m.t ?? m))).filter(Number.isFinite);

  fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, 'lyrics.json'), JSON.stringify(lyrics, null, 1) + '\n');
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, 'audio.json'), JSON.stringify(audio, null, 1) + '\n');
  console.log(`[mock_timing] source: ${source}`);
  console.log(`[mock_timing] bpm ${bpm}, ${beats.length} beats, ${sections.length} sections, ${lines.length} lines, duration ${duration.toFixed(2)}s`);
  for (const s of sections) console.log(`  ${s.name.padEnd(8)} ${s.start.toFixed(3).padStart(8)} - ${s.end.toFixed(3).padStart(8)}`);
  console.log(`[mock_timing] wrote ${path.relative(process.cwd(), outDir) || outDir}/lyrics.json, audio.json`);
}

build();

(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)
(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh shh shh)

[Verse 2]
Four a.m., you Google "is this normal" with the brightness low
Every tab says "normal." None of them say when it'll go
You whisper "teeth?" I've got eight and they're fine
"Gas?" I burped at nine. "A leap?" That's a horoscope with a timeline
Ferber says to check at three, then five, then ten. You lasted one
The chair method: you sat. I stood. We stared. Neither of us won
You bounce me down the hallway, hundred-twenty-eight BPM
That's where I got the beat. The untz is you. I'm just the stem

[Pre-Chorus - build, riser]
The monitor's on your nightstand. Volume: max. You hear everything
You hear everything. Here's how I reach you:

[Chorus - drop]
It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)
It's 3am and I'm crying (I'm crying)
It's 4am and you're crying
It's 11am and I'm napping

[Bridge - half-time, soft, music box, no kick]
It's 11am and I'm napping
Hippocampus humming. I'm consolidating:
the stairs, the word for "dog," your face at four
If I twitch, that's my brain, learning where my hands are
The group chat gets its post: "Baby asleep, 10:45"
And you? You could sleep. You're doing dishes
"Sleep when the baby sleeps." You chose dishes
I'll be up at twelve-ten. I will be perfect
And tonight we do it all again:

[Final Chorus - full drop, higher]
It's 2am and I'm crying (I'm crying)
It's 3am and I'm crying (I'm crying)
It's 4am and we're crying
It's 5:40 and... good morning!
It's 11am and I'm napping

[Outro - beat cuts to white noise, spoken, flat]
Baby awake. Eleven fifty-two.
(untz.)

 # clawdpop
 
-Suno + Claude Code music videos in the "I'm Upping My P(doom)" lineage.
+Suno + Claude Code music videos in the "I'm Upping My P(doom)" lineage (Suno "Claude-Pop"
+song → forced-aligned lyrics → every frame a deterministic function of song time, painted
+in a browser and exported through headless Chrome and ffmpeg).
 
-- `songs/2am-crying/` — untz-untz track from the POV of a baby ("It's 2am and I'm crying")
-- `songs/baby-facts/` — dense fact-rap about infant science (Netflix *Babies* and the literature behind it)
-- `shared/` — renderer / alignment tooling shared across songs
+## Songs
+
+- `songs/2am-crying/` — "2AM (And I'm Crying)": untz-untz Eurodance from the point of view of
+  a one-year-old. `LYRICS.md`, `TREATMENT.md`, `SUNO_RUNS.md`, `synth/` (procedural demo of
+  the backing track), `video/` (renderer scenes).
+- `songs/baby-facts/` — dense fact-rap about infant science (Netflix *Babies* and the
+  literature behind it). `CONCEPT.md`, `FACTS.md`.
+
+## Shared tooling
+
+- `shared/renderer/` — song-agnostic engine: timeline, karaoke band, HUD, headless-Chrome
+  frame export, ffmpeg encode.
+- `shared/align/` — audio analysis: Demucs vocal stem → CTC forced alignment (torchaudio
+  MMS_FA) cross-checked with mlx-whisper → `lyrics.json`; librosa beats/downbeats/sections →
+  `audio.json`.
+
+## Pipeline
+
+1. Lyrics first. Dense, every line carrying a reference; a fixed hook that escalates.
+2. Scope Suno runs: custom mode, lyrics with section markers, 2 to 3 style-tag variants.
+   Driven with the `suno` CLI (`~/.local/bin/suno`, unofficial, costs ~70 credits per v5.5
+   call). Auth is a manual one-time `suno auth --login`.
+3. Reification with Opus 5.5 agents: procedural synth demo of the track (so the renderer can
+   be built before Suno audio exists), renderer engine + plates, alignment pipeline.
+4. Align the keeper Suno clip, swap the mock timing for real timing, render 1080p60.
+
+Audio and video outputs are gitignored; code, specs, JSON timing and contact sheets are
+committed.

+## v2: bright and innocent
+
+Same chorus, different baby. No parenting jargon, no snark. This baby does not know what a
+clock is, only that when you say a number in the dark, you come. Crying isn't sadness; it's
+the one word it has that works at night. Musically brighter: major key, bells, toy piano,
+bubblegum Eurodance, a music-box intro. The escalation is joy instead of exhaustion:
+*I'm crying → you came → we're dancing → the sun came too → napping.*
+
+**[Intro]** *(music box, soft; a small yawn; the beat fades in)*
+> I woke up. It's dark. Hello?
+> *(untz. untz. untz. untz.)*
+
+**[Verse 1]**
+> The room is dark, the fan goes round, the ocean sound is on.
+> I opened up my eyes to check on you, and you were gone.
+> I don't know what a clock is, but you say numbers in the night,
+> And every number means you're coming, and you bring the light.
+> I learned to stand today! I need to show you right away.
+> I'm not sad. I'm just loud. Loud is the only word I can say.
+> The dark is big and I am small, and that's okay, because
+> I know a way to find you. I'll do it now. Here it goes:
+
+**[Chorus]**
+> It's 2am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
+> It's 3am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
+> It's 4am and you came!
+> It's 11am and I'm napping
+
+**[Post-Chorus]**
+> *(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh, I'm here)*
+> *(untz untz untz untz) (shh shh, I'm here)*
+
+**[Verse 2]**
+> You carry me along the hall like we are going somewhere.
+> We're not. We're going down the hall. I love it. I don't care.
+> You bounce, I bounce, the floor goes boom, the boom goes in my feet.
+> I think you made this song for me. I think this is the beat.
+> You hum the song that's not a song. I know it. It's my favorite.
+> I pointed at the moon. You looked! I did that. I'm the bravest.
+> Your face is close and tired and it is my favorite face.
+> I'd like to stay like this forever. Is forever a place?
+
+**[Pre-Chorus]**
+> You put me down. You tiptoe out. I count to one. And then
+> I know a way to find you. Here it goes again:
+
+**[Chorus]**
+> It's 2am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
+> It's 3am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
+> It's 4am and we're dancing!
+> It's 11am and I'm napping
+
+**[Bridge]** *(music box, half-time, sunlight)*
+> It's 11am and I'm napping.
+> I close my eyes and the room goes somewhere, and I go with it.
+> I dream about the stairs. I dream the word for "dog." I dream your face.
+> And when I wake up, you're still here. You're always here.
+> That's my favorite trick. You do it every time.
+> And tonight, when it gets dark, I'll check again:
+
+**[Final Chorus]**
+> It's 2am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
+> It's 3am and I'm crying *(I'm crying)*
+> It's 4am and we're dancing!
+> It's 5am and the sun came too!
+> It's 11am and I'm napping
+
+**[Outro]** *(soft; the beat stops; one small voice)*
+> Good morning. You're here.
+> *(untz.)*
+
+### What v2 keeps from the research
+
+- "I learned to stand today, I need to show you": babies really do practice new motor
+  skills at night, and it's one of the better-supported causes of wakings.
+- "I pointed at the moon. You looked!": pointing to share attention arrives around 12
+  months and is the first joint-attention milestone.
+- "I dream about the stairs": naps consolidate what was learned that day.
+- "You bounce, I bounce, the floor goes boom": the hallway bounce is the tempo.
+
+### Which to run
+
+Run both. v1 is the funnier video; v2 is the one that could make a grandparent cry. If
+only one, v2 is the safer song and v1 the safer video.
"""It's 2am and I'm Crying -- declarative song spec + CLI entry point.

    uv run --project songs/2am-crying/synth python songs/2am-crying/synth/song.py

renders out/2am_crying.wav (stereo, 44.1 kHz, 24-bit), out/song.json and the
10 s previews out/preview_chorus.wav and out/preview_bridge.wav.

Everything musical lives in this file: tempo, key, the hook melody, drum step
strings, chords per bar and the arrangement. The engine is in timeline.py
(spec -> timed events), instruments.py, fx.py, master.py and render.py.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from timeline import Section, chords_every, per_bar

TITLE = "It's 2am and I'm Crying"
BPM = 128
SR = 44100
KEY = "A minor (final chorus lifts to C major)"
TARGET_LUFS = -10.0
CEILING_DBTP = -1.2   # limiter ceiling; leaves margin so measured TP <= -1 dBTP

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- drums -----
# One string = one bar. N characters split the bar into N equal steps
# (16 chars = 16ths, 32 = 32nds). X accent, x normal, o ghost, . rest.
KICK = "x...x...x...x..."
CLAP = "....x.......x..."
OHAT = "..x...x...x...x."          # offbeat open hats
CHAT = ".o.o.o.o.o.o.o.o"          # closed hats on the e and a
CH8 = "o.o.o.o.o.o.o.o."           # bridge: soft straight 8ths
HALF = "........x......."          # half-time backbeat (beat 3)
UNTZ3 = "x...x...x......."         # "untz untz untz" fill on beats 1-3
ROLL4 = "x...x...x...x..."
ROLL8 = "x.x.x.x.x.x.x.x."
ROLL16 = "xxxxxxxxxxxxxxxx"
ROLL32_CUT = "X" * 24 + "." * 8    # 32nds for three beats, then silence
FILL = "............xxxx"

# bass: offbeat 8ths in verses, rolling 16ths (skip the kick) in the drop
BASS_OFF = "..x...x...x...x."
BASS_ROLL = ".xXx.xXx.xXx.xXx"
PAD_STAB = "x.....x.....x..."          # 1, 2&, 4: sparse verse stabs
ARP = "0.1.2.1.3.1.2.1."
ARP16 = "0121312101213121"
LULLABY = "0...1...2...1..."

# ------------------------------------------------------------ the hook -----
# (step16 relative to the line's downbeat, duration in 16ths, note, syllable)
# Scansion: "it's" is a pickup; TWO/THREE on beat 1, a-M on 1& and 2,
# and-I'm on 2& and 3, CRY on 4, -ing on 4&. Collision rule: line 1's pickup
# sits on the and-of-4 (8th) because the bar before it is free; lines 2 and 3
# follow a line whose "-ing" owns the and-of-4, so "-ing" shrinks to a 16th
# and "it's" moves to the "a" of 4. Line 3 has two extra syllables and
# stumbles into 16ths at the top of its bar. A minor pentatonic throughout
# (A C D E G), so the same melody also works over the C-major lift.
LINE1 = dict(line=1, lyric="It's 2am and I'm crying", notes=[
    (-2, 2, "E4", "it's"),
    (0, 2, "A4", "TWO"), (2, 2, "A4", "a"), (4, 2, "C5", "M"), (6, 2, "A4", "and"),
    (8, 4, "C5", "I'm"), (12, 2, "E5", "CRY"), (14, 1, "D5", "ing"),
])
LINE2 = dict(line=2, lyric="It's 3am and I'm crying", notes=[
    (-1, 1, "C5", "it's"),
    (0, 2, "C5", "THREE"), (2, 2, "C5", "a"), (4, 2, "D5", "M"), (6, 2, "C5", "and"),
    (8, 4, "D5", "I'm"), (12, 2, "A5", "CRY"), (14, 1, "G5", "ing"),
])
LINE3 = dict(line=3, lyric="It's 11am and I'm napping", notes=[
    (-1, 1, "E5", "it's"),
    (0, 1, "D5", "e"), (1, 1, "E5", "LEV"), (2, 1, "D5", "en"), (3, 1, "C5", "a"),
    (4, 2, "A4", "M"), (6, 2, "G4", "and"), (8, 4, "A4", "I'm"),
    (12, 2, "G4", "NAP"), (14, 2, "E4", "ping"),
])
# bar 4 of every hook phrase is a rest for the lead (the "untz" fill answers)
HOOK = dict(lines=[LINE1, LINE2, LINE3], bars=[0, 1, 2])

# Vowel (start, end) and consonants for the formant voice, keyed by syllable.
SYLLABLES = {
    "it's": dict(v=("I", "I"), coda="ts"),
    "two": dict(v=("u", "u"), onset="t"),
    "three": dict(v=("i", "i"), onset="th"),
    "a": dict(v=("E", "i")),

    "m": dict(v=("E", "@")),

    "cry": dict(v=("a", "E"), onset="k", cry=True),   # the "waaah"
    "ing": dict(v=("I", "I")),
    "e": dict(v=("I", "I")),
    "lev": dict(v=("E", "E")),
    "en": dict(v=("@", "@")),
    "nap": dict(v=("ae", "ae")),
    "ping": dict(v=("I", "I"), onset="p"),
}


def hook_at(bars, voices=("cry", "saw"), **kw):
    """Place the 3-line hook phrase at each bar offset in `bars` for each voice."""
    out = []
    for b in bars:
        for v in voices:
            out.append(dict(HOOK, bars=[b + x for x in HOOK["bars"]], voice=v, **kw))
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------- arrangement -----
VERSE_CHORDS = ["Am", "F", "C", "G"]
DROP_CHORDS = ["Am", "F", "C", "G"]
LIFT_CHORDS = ["C", "G", "Am", "F"]          # final chorus: hopeful C-major axis
BUILD_CHORDS = ["F", "F", "G", "G", "Am", "Am", "E", "E"]


def build(name: str) -> Section:
    return Section(
        name, 8, BUILD_CHORDS, label="pre-chorus / build",
        drums=dict(
            kick=per_bar(8, KICK, b5_8=None),
            clap=per_bar(8, CLAP, b5_8=None),
            ohat=per_bar(8, OHAT, b7_8=None),
            snare=per_bar(8, ROLL4, b3_4=ROLL8, b5_7=ROLL16, b8=ROLL32_CUT),
        ),
        drum_gain=dict(snare=(0.25, 1.0), ohat=0.8),
        bass=dict(pattern=per_bar(8, BASS_OFF, b5_8=None)),
        sub=False,
        pad=dict(gain=(0.55, 0.9), cutoff=[(0, 500), (8, 7000)]),
        arp=dict(voice="pluck", pattern=per_bar(8, ARP, b5_7=ARP16, b8=ARP16[:12] + "...."), gain=(0.6, 1.0)),
        fx=[("riser", 0, 8 - 0.25), ("revcym", 7, 1)],
        cut_last_beats=1.0,
    )


def chorus(name: str, chords: list[str], final: bool = False) -> Section:
    voices = ("cry", "saw", "saw_hi") if final else ("cry", "saw")
    return Section(
        name, 16, chords_every(chords, 1, 16), label="final chorus (C-major lift)" if final else "chorus / drop",
        drums=dict(
            kick=per_bar(16, KICK),
            clap=per_bar(16, CLAP),
            ohat=per_bar(16, OHAT),
            chat=per_bar(16, CHAT),
            untz=per_bar(16, None, b4=UNTZ3, b8=UNTZ3, b12=UNTZ3, b16=UNTZ3),
            snare=per_bar(16, None, b16=FILL),
        ),
        drum_gain=dict(untz=0.9),
        bass=dict(pattern=BASS_ROLL, gate=0.9),
        pad=dict(gain=1.0, cutoff=[(0, 9000)]),
        melody=hook_at([0, 4, 8, 12], voices),
        fx=[("crash", 0, 1), ("crash", 8, 1, 0.6)] + ([("crash", 4, 1, 0.5), ("crash", 12, 1, 0.5)] if final else []),
    )


SECTIONS = [
    Section("intro", 4, chords_every(VERSE_CHORDS, 1, 4), label="intro",
            drums=dict(ohat=per_bar(4, OHAT), chat=per_bar(4, None, b3_4=CHAT)),

                       chat=per_bar(8, CHAT), untz=per_bar(8, None, b4=UNTZ3, b8=UNTZ3)),

            fx=[("riser", 6, 2 - 0.25, 0.9), ("revcym", 7, 1)],
            cut_last_beats=1.0),
    chorus("final", LIFT_CHORDS, final=True),
    Section("outro", 4, ["C", "G", "Am", "Am"], label="outro",
            drums=dict(untz=per_bar(4, None, b4="x...............")),
            drum_gain=dict(untz=0.8),
            pad=dict(gain=(0.8, 0.0), cutoff=[(0, 5000), (4, 600)]),
            melody=[dict(lines=[LINE1], bars=[0], stretch=2, voice="celesta", transpose=12, gain=0.8)],
            fx=[("crash", 0, 1, 0.7)]),
]


def main(argv=None):
    import argparse
    from pathlib import Path

    from render import render_song

    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "out"))
    ap.add_argument("--no-previews", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    render_song(Path(a.out), previews=not a.no_previews)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/tools/make_click.mjs
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Placeholder audio: a 128 BPM click + kick track that follows data/audio.json, so the
// preview and the render pipeline can be tested end to end before the synth demo or the
// Suno vocal exists. Written directly as a 16-bit WAV (no external deps); deterministic.
//
//   node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/make_click.mjs [--out songs/2am-crying/video/audio/click.wav]
//
// Layers: kick on audio.kicks, a soft tick on every beat, offbeat hats on audio.hats,
// white noise in the intro/outro, a noise riser on audio.risers, a muffled thump and a
// music-box arpeggio in the bridge, near-silence on audio.quiet.

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const VIDEO = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const out = args.includes('--out') ? path.resolve(args[args.indexOf('--out') + 1]) : path.join(VIDEO, 'audio', 'click.wav');
const A = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(VIDEO, 'data', 'audio.json'), 'utf8'));

const SR = 44100;
const dur = A.duration + 0.75;
const N = Math.ceil(dur * SR);
const buf = new Float32Array(N);

// deterministic noise
let seed = 0x2a2a2a;
const rnd = () => { seed = (seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0; return seed / 4294967296 * 2 - 1; };
const noise = new Float32Array(SR * 2);
for (let i = 0; i < noise.length; i++) noise[i] = rnd();

const sec = (name) => A.sections.find((s) => s.name === name);
const inSpans = (t, spans) => (spans || []).some((q) => t >= q.start && t < q.end);
const beatDur = 60 / A.bpm;

function add(i, v) { if (i >= 0 && i < N) buf[i] += v; }

function kick(t, gain = 1) {
  const i0 = Math.round(t * SR), L = Math.round(0.35 * SR);
  let ph = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < L; k++) {
    const tt = k / SR;
    const f = 48 + 110 * Math.exp(-tt * 28);
    ph += (2 * Math.PI * f) / SR;
    const env = Math.exp(-tt * 9) * Math.min(1, k / 40);
    add(i0 + k, gain * 0.85 * Math.sin(ph) * env + gain * 0.15 * noise[k % noise.length] * Math.exp(-tt * 120));
  }
}
function tick(t, gain = 0.12, f = 2400) {
  const i0 = Math.round(t * SR), L = Math.round(0.03 * SR);
  for (let k = 0; k < L; k++) add(i0 + k, gain * Math.sin((2 * Math.PI * f * k) / SR) * Math.exp(-k / SR * 160));
}
function hat(t, gain = 0.18) {
  const i0 = Math.round(t * SR), L = Math.round(0.09 * SR);
  let prev = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < L; k++) {
    const n = noise[(i0 + k) % noise.length];
    const hp = n - prev; prev = n; // crude high-pass
    add(i0 + k, gain * hp * Math.exp(-k / SR * 38));
  }
}
function bell(t, midi, gain = 0.16) {
  const f = 440 * Math.pow(2, (midi - 69) / 12);
  const i0 = Math.round(t * SR), L = Math.round(1.2 * SR);
  for (let k = 0; k < L; k++) {
    const tt = k / SR;
    const env = Math.exp(-tt * 3.2) * Math.min(1, k / 60);
    add(i0 + k, gain * env * (Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f * tt) + 0.35 * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * f * 2.76 * tt) * Math.exp(-tt * 6)));
  }
}
function noiseBed(a, b, gain, fadeIn = 0.5, fadeOut = 0.5) {
  const i0 = Math.round(a * SR), i1 = Math.round(b * SR);
  let lp = 0;
  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
    const n = noise[i % noise.length];
    lp += 0.25 * (n - lp); // soft low-pass: "shower" white noise
    const tt = (i - i0) / SR, rem = (i1 - i) / SR;
    const env = Math.min(1, tt / fadeIn, rem / fadeOut);
    add(i, gain * lp * env);
  }
}
function riser(a, b, gain = 0.22) {
  const i0 = Math.round(a * SR), i1 = Math.round(b * SR);
  let lp = 0;
  for (let i = i0; i < i1; i++) {
    const x = (i - i0) / (i1 - i0);
    const c = 0.02 + 0.6 * x * x;
    lp += c * (noise[i % noise.length] - lp);
    add(i, gain * x * x * lp);
  }
}

// --- layers
const intro = sec('intro'), outro = sec('outro'), bridge = sec('bridge');
if (intro) noiseBed(intro.start, intro.end, 0.35, 0.8, 0.3);
if (outro) noiseBed(outro.start, outro.end + 0.6, 0.35, 0.05, 1.5);
for (const r of A.risers || []) riser(r.start, r.end);

for (const k of A.kicks) kick(k, 1);
const hats = A.hats || [];
for (const h of hats) hat(h);
A.beats.forEach((b, i) => {
  if (inSpans(b, A.quiet)) return;
  if (intro && b < intro.end && b < intro.start + (intro.end - intro.start) / 2) return;
  if (outro && b >= outro.start) return;
  tick(b, i % (A.beats_per_bar || 4) === 0 ? 0.16 : 0.08, i % 4 === 0 ? 2600 : 2000);
});
if (bridge) {
  // muffled club through the wall + music box hook (A minor pentatonic)
  const hook = [69, 69, 72, 69, 72, 76, 74, 72];
  A.beats.forEach((b) => {
    if (b < bridge.start || b >= bridge.end) return;
    kick(b, 0.18);
  });
  let k = 0;
  for (let t = bridge.start; t < bridge.end - beatDur; t += beatDur, k++) {
    if (k % 2 === 0) bell(t, hook[(k / 2) % hook.length] + 12, 0.12);
  }
}
// near-silence: duck everything except a faint noise bed
for (const q of A.quiet || []) {
  const bar = beatDur * (A.beats_per_bar || 4);
  const a = Math.round((q.end - bar) * SR), b = Math.round(q.end * SR);
  for (let i = a; i < b && i < N; i++) buf[i] *= 0.06;
}

// --- normalize + write 16-bit stereo WAV
let peak = 0;
for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) peak = Math.max(peak, Math.abs(buf[i]));
const g = peak > 0 ? 0.89 / peak : 1;
const data = Buffer.alloc(N * 4);
for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
  const v = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, buf[i] * g));
  const s = Math.round(v * 32767);
  data.writeInt16LE(s, i * 4);
  data.writeInt16LE(s, i * 4 + 2);
}
const hdr = Buffer.alloc(44);
hdr.write('RIFF', 0); hdr.writeUInt32LE(36 + data.length, 4); hdr.write('WAVE', 8);
hdr.write('fmt ', 12); hdr.writeUInt32LE(16, 16); hdr.writeUInt16LE(1, 20); hdr.writeUInt16LE(2, 22);
hdr.writeUInt32LE(SR, 24); hdr.writeUInt32LE(SR * 4, 28); hdr.writeUInt16LE(4, 32); hdr.writeUInt16LE(16, 34);
hdr.write('data', 36); hdr.writeUInt32LE(data.length, 40);
fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(out, Buffer.concat([hdr, data]));
console.log(`[make_click] wrote ${out} (${dur.toFixed(2)}s, source timing: ${A.source || 'unknown'})`);
result
File created successfully at: /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/tools/make_click.mjs (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)

// Math, easing, envelopes and seeded hashing. Everything here is pure: a frame is a
// function of song time, so nothing may carry state between calls.

export const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
export const clamp = (x, a = 0, b = 1) => (x < a ? a : x > b ? b : x);
export const lerp = (a, b, k) => a + (b - a) * k;
export const invLerp = (a, b, x) => (b === a ? 0 : (x - a) / (b - a));
/** Progress of t through [a, b], clamped to 0..1. */
export const seg = (t, a, b) => clamp(invLerp(a, b, t));
export const smooth = (k) => { k = clamp(k); return k * k * (3 - 2 * k); };
/** 1 inside [a, b], with linear fades of fi / fo seconds at the edges. */
export const trap = (t, a, b, fi = 0.1, fo = 0.1) =>
  Math.min(fi > 0 ? clamp((t - a) / fi) : (t >= a ? 1 : 0), fo > 0 ? clamp((b - t) / fo) : (t < b ? 1 : 0));

export const ease = {
  linear: (k) => k,
  inQuad: (k) => k * k,
  outQuad: (k) => 1 - (1 - k) * (1 - k),
  inOutQuad: (k) => (k < 0.5 ? 2 * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 2) / 2),
  inCubic: (k) => k * k * k,
  outCubic: (k) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3),
  inOutCubic: (k) => (k < 0.5 ? 4 * k * k * k : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * k + 2, 3) / 2),
  outQuart: (k) => 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 4),
  inQuart: (k) => k * k * k * k,
  inExpo: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : Math.pow(2, 10 * k - 10)),
  outExpo: (k) => (k >= 1 ? 1 : 1 - Math.pow(2, -10 * k)),
  inOutExpo: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : k >= 1 ? 1 : k < 0.5 ? Math.pow(2, 20 * k - 10) / 2 : (2 - Math.pow(2, -20 * k + 10)) / 2),
  outBack: (k, s = 1.70158) => 1 + (s + 1) * Math.pow(k - 1, 3) + s * Math.pow(k - 1, 2),
  inBack: (k, s = 1.70158) => (s + 1) * k * k * k - s * k * k,
  outElastic: (k) => (k <= 0 ? 0 : k >= 1 ? 1 : Math.pow(2, -10 * k) * Math.sin((k * 10 - 0.75) * (TAU / 3)) + 1),
  outBounce: (k) => {
    const n = 7.5625, d = 2.75;
    if (k < 1 / d) return n * k * k;
    if (k < 2 / d) return n * (k -= 1.5 / d) * k + 0.75;
    if (k < 2.5 / d) return n * (k -= 2.25 / d) * k + 0.9375;
    return n * (k -= 2.625 / d) * k + 0.984375;
  },
};
/** Eased progress of t through [a, b]. */
export const eseg = (t, a, b, fn = ease.outCubic) => fn(seg(t, a, b));

/**
 * Envelope that peaks (=1) at `at`: linear attack over `attack` seconds before it,
 * exponential decay with time constant `decay` after it. 0 far away.
 */
export function pulse(t, at, attack = 0.0, decay = 0.2) {
  const d = t - at;
  if (d < 0) return attack > 0 && d > -attack ? 1 + d / attack : 0;
  return Math.exp(-d / decay);
}

/** Integer hash -> [0, 1). Stable across runs and machines (Mulberry32 finalizer). */
export function hash(i, salt = 0) {
  let x = (Math.imul((i | 0) ^ 0x9e3779b9, 0x85ebca6b) ^ Math.imul(salt | 0, 0xc2b2ae35)) >>> 0;
  x = Math.imul(x ^ (x >>> 16), 0x7feb352d) >>> 0;
  x = Math.imul(x ^ (x >>> 15), 0x846ca68b) >>> 0;
  x = (x ^ (x >>> 16)) >>> 0;
  return x / 4294967296;
}
/** Signed hash in [-1, 1). */
export const shash = (i, salt = 0) => hash(i, salt) * 2 - 1;
/** Hash of a string (for naming seeds by object id). */
export function strSeed(s) {
  let h = 2166136261;
  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) h = Math.imul(h ^ s.charCodeAt(i), 16777619);
  return h >>> 0;
}
/** A deterministic generator for loops inside one frame (seed it per object, never per frame order). */
export function rng(seed) {
  let a = seed >>> 0 || 1;
  return () => {
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
    let t = a;
    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
/** Smooth 1D value noise in [-1, 1]. */
export function noise1(x, salt = 0) {
  const i = Math.floor(x), f = x - i;
  const u = f * f * (3 - 2 * f);
  return lerp(shash(i, salt), shash(i + 1, salt), u);
}

/** Index of the last element of sorted `arr` that is <= x (-1 if none). */
export function bisect(arr, x, key = null) {
  let lo = 0, hi = arr.length - 1, ans = -1;
  while (lo <= hi) {
    const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
    const v = key ? key(arr[mid]) : arr[mid];
    if (v <= x) { ans = mid; lo = mid + 1; } else hi = mid - 1;
  }
  return ans;
}

/** True when the event at time `ev` belongs to the frame sampled at t (interval [t, t+1/fps)). */
export const inFrame = (ev, t, fps) => ev >= t - 1e-9 && ev < t + 1 / fps - 1e-9;

// Beat grid helpers built from data/audio.json.
//   beatPos(t)   -> { beat (fractional index), i, phase, bar, barPhase, beatInBar }
//   beatTime(fi) -> seconds at a fractional beat index (extrapolates past the grid)
//   snap(t, mode) with mode 'beat' | 'half' | 'downbeat' | 'none'
//   kickEnv / sinceKick / hatEnv for strobes; sectionAt(t)

import { bisect, clamp } from './util.js';

export function makeBeats(audio) {
  const bpm = audio.bpm || 120;
  const beatDur = 60 / bpm;
  const bpb = audio.beats_per_bar || 4;
  const beats = (audio.beats && audio.beats.length ? audio.beats : [0]).slice();
  const downbeats = audio.downbeats && audio.downbeats.length ? audio.downbeats.slice() : beats.filter((_, i) => i % bpb === 0);
  const kicks = (audio.kicks || []).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const hats = (audio.hats && audio.hats.length ? audio.hats : beats.map((b) => b + beatDur / 2)).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const sections = (audio.sections || []).slice();
  const duration = audio.duration || (beats.at(-1) + beatDur);
  // index of the first downbeat in the beat array, so bar numbers follow the downbeats
  const db0 = Math.max(0, beats.findIndex((b) => Math.abs(b - downbeats[0]) < beatDur / 4));

  function beatTime(fi) {
    const n = beats.length;
    if (fi <= 0) return beats[0] + fi * beatDur;
    if (fi >= n - 1) return beats[n - 1] + (fi - (n - 1)) * beatDur;
    const i = Math.floor(fi), f = fi - i;
    return beats[i] + f * (beats[i + 1] - beats[i]);
  }
  function beatIndex(t) {
    const n = beats.length;
    if (t <= beats[0]) return (t - beats[0]) / beatDur;
    if (t >= beats[n - 1]) return n - 1 + (t - beats[n - 1]) / beatDur;
    const i = bisect(beats, t);
    return i + (t - beats[i]) / (beats[i + 1] - beats[i]);
  }
  function beatPos(t) {
    const beat = beatIndex(t);
    const i = Math.floor(beat);
    const phase = beat - i;
    const rel = beat - db0;
    const bar = Math.floor(rel / bpb);
    const barPhase = (rel / bpb) - bar;
    const beatInBar = ((i - db0) % bpb + bpb) % bpb;
    return { beat, i, phase, bar, barPhase, beatInBar };
  }
  function snap(t, mode = 'downbeat') {
    if (mode === 'none' || !mode) return t;
    const b = beatIndex(t);
    if (mode === 'beat') return beatTime(Math.round(b));
    if (mode === 'half') return beatTime(Math.round(b * 2) / 2);
    if (mode === 'downbeat') {
      const rel = (b - db0) / bpb;
      return beatTime(db0 + Math.round(rel) * bpb);
    }
    if (mode === 'prevDownbeat') {
      const rel = (b - db0 + 1e-6) / bpb;
      return beatTime(db0 + Math.floor(rel) * bpb);
    }
    throw new Error('unknown snap mode ' + mode);
  }
  const barStart = (t) => snap(t, 'prevDownbeat');
  const barDur = beatDur * bpb;

  function lastKick(t) {
    const i = bisect(kicks, t + 1e-9);
    return i < 0 ? -Infinity : kicks[i];
  }
  const sinceKick = (t) => t - lastKick(t);
  /** 1 at a kick, decaying exponentially. */
  const kickEnv = (t, decay = 0.12) => { const d = sinceKick(t); return d < 0 || !isFinite(d) ? 0 : Math.exp(-d / decay); };
  function kickIndex(t) { return bisect(kicks, t + 1e-9); }
  function lastHat(t) { const i = bisect(hats, t + 1e-9); return i < 0 ? -Infinity : hats[i]; }
  const hatEnv = (t, decay = 0.06) => { const d = t - lastHat(t); return !isFinite(d) ? 0 : Math.exp(-d / decay); };
  /** Beat envelope independent of kicks (works in kick-less sections). */
  const beatEnv = (t, decay = 0.12) => { const p = beatPos(t); return Math.exp(-(p.phase * beatDur) / decay); };

  function sectionAt(t) {
    for (const s of sections) if (t >= s.start && t < s.end) return s;
    return t < (sections[0]?.start ?? 0) ? sections[0] : sections.at(-1);
  }
  const section = (name) => sections.find((s) => s.name === name);
  const inSpans = (t, spans) => (spans || []).some((q) => t >= q.start && t < q.end);

  return {
    bpm, beatDur, barDur, bpb, beats, downbeats, kicks, hats, sections, duration,
    beatTime, beatIndex, beatPos, snap, barStart, lastKick, sinceKick, kickEnv, kickIndex,
    lastHat, hatEnv, beatEnv, sectionAt, section, inSpans,
    quiet: audio.quiet || [], risers: audio.risers || [],
    /** Progress through a riser span covering t (0 if none). */
    riser(t) { for (const r of audio.risers || []) if (t >= r.start && t < r.end) return clamp((t - r.start) / (r.end - r.start)); return 0; },
  };
}
