// Crib at night. Chorus: 2am / 3am / 4am crying, the same framing with the parent
// silhouette progressing (absent -> in the lit doorway -> sitting on the floor by the crib).
// Verse 2 modes: quiet (5am: nearly black, two open eyes), fake (7:45 "sleeping": eyes wide,
// a cut-in of the parents' IR monitor view), sleep (8pm-12am: eyes closed, the phone
// drifting, darker and stiller), void (1am: black, record dot, the eyes open in the silence).
import { drawBaby, babyColors } from '../baby.js';
import { wall, cribBack, doorway, parent, spill, fillAll } from '../props.js';
import { FLASH } from '../tod.js';
import { drawMonitorCutIn } from '../monitor.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, flashFalloff, flashPop, CW, CH } from './common.js';

const HALL = '#ffc97e';

function nightRoom(ctx, C, { door = 0, sit = 0, stand = 0 }) {
  const { G } = C;
  wall(ctx, G, { glow: 0 });
  if (door > 0) {
    const d = doorway(ctx, G, { x: 700, y: 150, w: 300, h: 720, open: 0.75 * door, hall: HALL, hallAlpha: door });
    spill(ctx, d.x, d.y + 300, d.w, 600, HALL, 0.18 * door);
    if (stand > 0) {
      ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = stand;
      parent(ctx, { x: 790, y: 250, s: 52, pose: 'stand', color: '#0d0e14', rim: rgba(HALL, 0.55) });
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }
  if (sit > 0) {
    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = sit;
    parent(ctx, { x: 880, y: 770, s: 78, pose: 'sit', color: '#16171f', rim: rgba(HALL, 0.4), eyes: 'tired', eyeColor: rgba('#d8dbe2', 0.85), lean: -0.12 });
    ctx.restore();
  }
  cribBack(ctx, G, { railY: 700, bottomY: 1500 });
}

export default {
  camera(C) {
    const p = C.params;
    if (p.mode === 'void' || (p.mode === 'sleep' && (p.dark || 0) >= 2)) return { still: true, grain: 1.4 };
    if (p.mode === 'sleep') return { shake: 0.5, kick: 0.3, tilt: 0.05 * Math.sin(C.lt * 0.9) + 0.03, zoom: 1 + 0.03 * C.lt };
    if (p.mode === 'quiet') return { shake: 0.3, kick: 0.2, grain: 1.5 };
    if (p.stage === 1) return { tilt: 0.26 };
    if (p.stage === 2) return { tilt: -0.05 };
    return {};
  },
  caption(C) {
    const p = C.params;
    if (p.stage != null) return { slam: ['crying'] };
    return {};
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const p = C.params;
    const { G, lt } = C;
    if (p.mode === 'void' || p.mode === 'quiet') {
      fillAll(ctx, p.mode === 'void' ? '#000000' : '#04050a');
      // two open eyes in the dark
      const eyesAt = p.mode === 'void' ? C.B.snap(C.end - C.B.barDur, 'downbeat') : C.start + 0.12;
      const k = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, eyesAt, eyesAt + 0.25));
      if (k > 0) {
        if (p.mode === 'quiet') {
          ctx.globalAlpha = 0.16;
          drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(G, 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'wide', mouth: 'none' });
          ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        }
        for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
          const ex = SELFIE.x + s * 0.35 * SELFIE.r, ey = SELFIE.y + 0.02 * SELFIE.r;
          const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(ex, ey, 2, ex, ey, 46);
          g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#e8eeff', 0.9 * k));
          g.addColorStop(0.35, rgba('#c9d4f0', 0.35 * k));
          g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#c9d4f0', 0));
          ctx.fillStyle = g;
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex, ey, 46, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
          ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#05060c', k);
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex, ey, 15, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
          ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', k);
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex + 6, ey - 6, 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
        }
      }
      return;
    }
    if (p.mode === 'sleep') {
      const dark = p.dark || 0;
      nightRoom(ctx, C, {});
      drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(G, 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'line', free: { mode: 'down' } });
      // a dim night-light glow on the face, dimmer each hour
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(SELFIE.x - 200, SELFIE.y - 100, 40, SELFIE.x, SELFIE.y, SELFIE.r * 2.2);
      g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ff5a3c', 0.16 / (1 + dark)));
      g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ff5a3c', 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = g;
      ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
      ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#000006', 0.25 + 0.22 * dark);
      ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
      return;
    }
    if (p.mode === 'fake') {
      nightRoom(ctx, C, {});
      drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(FLASH, 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'wide', mouth: 'line', look: [0, 0], flash: 0.6 });
      flashFalloff(ctx, SELFIE.x, SELFIE.y, SELFIE.r, 0.9);
      flashPop(ctx, lt, 0.4);
      // the parents' monitor view cut in for the second half of the clip
      const cutA = C.start + C.dur * 0.5;
      if (C.t >= cutA) drawMonitorCutIn(ctx, C, C.t - cutA);
      return;
    }
    // chorus crying: stage 0 / 1 / 2
    const st = p.stage || 0;
    const appear = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.15, C.start + C.dur * 0.6));
    nightRoom(ctx, C, { door: st >= 1 ? 1 : 0, stand: st === 1 ? appear : 0, sit: st === 2 ? 1 : 0 });
    drawBaby(ctx, {
      ...SELFIE, C: babyColors(FLASH, 'sack'), outfit: 'sack', eyes: 'squeeze', mouth: 'o',
      mouthOpen: 0.85 + 0.15 * Math.sin(C.t * 21), tears: (lt * 0.9) % 1, flash: 0.75, blush: 0.35,
      free: { mode: 'down' },
    });
    flashFalloff(ctx, SELFIE.x, SELFIE.y, SELFIE.r, 0.78);
    flashPop(ctx, lt, 0.5);
  },
};

// The v1 look, kept as a joke for the 7:45pm "sleeping" clip: the parents' IR baby-monitor
// view cut in over the selfie. IR greyscale, top-down crib, the baby lying down with two
// glowing eyes wide open, "NURSERY CAM • 07:45 PM" and a green LIVE.

import { hash, TAU } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { rgba } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { CW, CH } from './props.js';

export const IR = { bg: '#0b0b0e', dim: '#2c2f36', mid: '#5a5e66', soft: '#8b9098', ink: '#c9ccd1', hi: '#f2f4f7', red: '#ff2a2a', green: '#39ff7a' };

export function drawMonitorCutIn(ctx, C, dt) {
  const P = IR;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.setTransform(ctx.getTransform()); // keep the camera transform; the cut-in fills the frame
  ctx.fillStyle = P.bg;
  ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
  // top-down crib: mattress, slats on all four sides
  const mx = 140, my = 420, mw = 800, mh = 1180;
  ctx.fillStyle = '#1b1d22';
  ctx.fillRect(mx, my, mw, mh);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.dim;
  for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) { ctx.fillRect(mx - 40 + i * (mw + 80) / 10, my - 50, 16, 40); ctx.fillRect(mx - 40 + i * (mw + 80) / 10, my + mh + 10, 16, 40); }
  for (let i = 0; i <= 14; i++) { ctx.fillRect(mx - 50, my - 40 + i * (mh + 80) / 14, 40, 16); ctx.fillRect(mx + mw + 10, my - 40 + i * (mh + 80) / 14, 40, 16); }
  ctx.strokeStyle = P.mid; ctx.lineWidth = 10;
  ctx.strokeRect(mx - 30, my - 30, mw + 60, mh + 60);
  // the baby lying on its back, in a sack, arms up by the head
  const bx = 540, by = 880;
  ctx.fillStyle = P.mid;
  rrect(ctx, bx - 190, by + 120, 380, 560, 150); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = P.soft;
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(bx, by, 170, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(bx + k * 230, by + 40, 50, 120, k * 0.5, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  // IR eyes: wide open, glowing
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) {
    const ex = bx + k * 60, ey = by + 10;
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(ex, ey, 2, ex, ey, 60);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)');
    g.addColorStop(0.25, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ex, ey, 60, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  }
  // scanlines + grain
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)';
  for (let y = 0; y < CH; y += 8) ctx.fillRect(0, y, CW, 3);
  const tick = Math.floor(C.t * 24);
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
  for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) ctx.fillRect(hash(i, tick) * CW, hash(i, tick + 9) * CH, 4, 4);
  // HUD
  ctx.font = '600 44px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText('NURSERY CAM  •  07:45 PM', 60, 1780);
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  ctx.fillStyle = P.green;
  ctx.fillText('LIVE', CW - 60, 1780);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(CW - 60 - ctx.measureText('LIVE').width - 26, 1766, 12, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.font = '600 38px "IBM Plex Mono", monospace';
  ctx.fillText('STATUS: ASLEEP', CW / 2, 300);
  ctx.restore();
}

// "2AM (And I'm Crying)", v3 "the day": the baby's selfie video. Song module for the
// shared renderer. Every frame: map the outro onto the intro (the loop), build a clip
// descriptor, draw the camera frame (clip under the camera transform -> lens post ->
// lens overlays -> caption -> status bar), then lay it out: full frame (9:16) or inside a
// phone on a soft nursery backdrop (16:9).

import { drawCaption } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/caption.js';
import { rgba, mix } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { clamp, TAU } from '../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { timeline } from './clips.js';
import { makeStory } from './story.js';
import { grade, skyBody } from './tod.js';
import { drawStatusBar } from './statusbar.js';
import { cameraTransform, lensPost } from './camera.js';
import { CW, CH } from './props.js';
import hello from './scenes/hello.js';
import closeup from './scenes/closeup.js';
import crib from './scenes/crib.js';

export const FONTS = [
  { family: 'Archivo Black', weight: 400 },
  { family: 'IBM Plex Mono', weight: 500 }, { family: 'IBM Plex Mono', weight: 600 }, { family: 'IBM Plex Mono', weight: 700 },
  { family: 'Inter', weight: 700 }, { family: 'Inter', weight: 900 }, { family: 'Inter', weight: 900, style: 'italic' },
  { family: 'Nunito', weight: 900 },
];

export const scenes = {
  hello, closeup, crib,
  rear: closeup, nap: closeup, pooping: closeup, highchair: closeup, standing: closeup, bath: closeup, fan: closeup, grid: closeup,
};

export const CAPTION = { x: 540, y: 560, maxW: 900, size: 88, minSize: 56, family: '"Inter", sans-serif', weight: 900 };

/** Words the caption shows at t for this window. */
function captionWords(env, w, t) {
  const st = env.story;
  if (w.plate === 'hello') return w.lines.flatMap((l) => l.words);
  const c = st.clauseAt(t, w);
  return c ? c.words : [];
}

/** Clip descriptor: everything a clip type needs, nothing read from global state. */
export function makeClip(env, t, w, extra = {}) {
  const st = env.story;
  const min = extra.min ?? st.clock(t);
  const G = grade(min);
  return {
    type: w.plate, params: w.params, win: w, t, lt: t - w.start, dur: w.dur, start: w.start, end: w.end,
    min, G, night: G.light.night > 0.5, words: captionWords(env, w, t), clause: st.clauseAt(t, w),
    B: env.B, L: env.L, env, fps: env.fps, story: st, tile: false, ...extra,
  };
}

/** Draw one camera frame (1080x1920 camera space) for clip C. */
export function drawCameraFrame(ctx, C, { chrome = true } = {}) {
  const scene = scenes[C.type];
  const cam = scene.camera?.(C) || {};
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(0, 0, CW, CH); ctx.clip();
  ctx.fillStyle = C.G.wall;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
  ctx.save();
  cameraTransform(ctx, C, C.tile ? { ...cam, still: true } : cam);
  scene.draw(ctx, C);
  ctx.restore();
  lensPost(ctx, C, cam);
  if (scene.overlay) { ctx.save(); scene.overlay(ctx, C); ctx.restore(); }
  if (chrome) {
    const capOpts = scene.caption ? scene.caption(C) : {};
    if (capOpts !== false) drawCaption(ctx, C.t, C.words, { ...CAPTION, ...capOpts });
    const st = C.story;
    drawStatusBar(ctx, { min: C.min, battery: st.battery(C.t), rec: st.rec(C.t) });
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

// ---- 16:9: the phone centred on a soft, out-of-focus nursery ---------------------------
const PHONE = { sx: 690, sy: 60, sw: 540, sh: 960, bezel: 16, r: 64 };

function drawBackdrop(ctx, C) {
  const G = C.G;
  const W = 1920, H = 1080;
  ctx.fillStyle = G.wall;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  const night = G.light.night;
  // window light (left), soft
  const wx = 330, wy = 380;
  let g = ctx.createRadialGradient(wx, wy, 40, wx, wy, 520);
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(mix(G.window, '#ffffff', 0.3), 0.85 * (1 - night) + 0.1));
  g.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(G.window, 0.35 * (1 - night) + 0.04));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.window, 0));
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  // crib slats (right), out of focus
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
    const x = 1360 + i * 62;
    const sg = ctx.createLinearGradient(x - 26, 0, x + 26, 0);
    sg.addColorStop(0, rgba(G.crib, 0));
    sg.addColorStop(0.5, rgba(G.crib, 0.55));
    sg.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.crib, 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = sg;
    ctx.fillRect(x - 26, 470, 52, 520);
  }
  ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.crib, 0.4);
  ctx.fillRect(1310, 440, 600, 40);
  // floor
  g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 900, 0, H);
  g.addColorStop(0, rgba(G.floor, 0));
  g.addColorStop(1, rgba(G.floor, 0.9));
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 900, W, 180);
  // a lamp bokeh at night
  if (night > 0.3) {
    for (const [bx, by, br] of [[260, 760, 70], [330, 700, 40], [1700, 300, 55]]) {
      const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(bx, by, 0, bx, by, br);
      bg.addColorStop(0, rgba('#ffcf8a', 0.35 * night));
      bg.addColorStop(1, rgba('#ffcf8a', 0));
      ctx.fillStyle = bg;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(bx, by, br, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
  }
  // the sun / moon arc (top right)
  const ax = 1660, ay = 230, ar = 150;
  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(night > 0.5 ? '#c9d3ff' : '#ffffff', 0.45);
  ctx.lineWidth = 3;
  ctx.setLineDash([4, 12]);
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(ax, ay, ar, Math.PI, TAU); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  const sb = skyBody(C.min);
  const a = Math.PI + sb.p * Math.PI;
  const px = ax + Math.cos(a) * ar, py = ay + Math.sin(a) * ar;
  ctx.fillStyle = sb.body === 'sun' ? '#ffd36b' : '#e8ecff';
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px, py, 18, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  if (sb.body === 'moon') { ctx.fillStyle = G.wall; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(px + 8, py - 6, 15, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); }
  // soft vignette
  g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 300, W / 2, H / 2, 1150);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(0,0,0,${0.35 + 0.3 * night})`);
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
}

function drawPhone(ctx, C) {
  const p = PHONE;
  // shadow
  const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sw * 0.3, p.sx + p.sw / 2, p.sy + p.sh * 0.55, p.sh * 0.75);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)');
  g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = g;
  ctx.fillRect(p.sx - 400, p.sy - 200, p.sw + 800, p.sh + 400);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#0d0d10';
  rrect(ctx, p.sx - p.bezel, p.sy - p.bezel, p.sw + 2 * p.bezel, p.sh + 2 * p.bezel, p.r + p.bezel);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.save();
  rrect(ctx, p.sx, p.sy, p.sw, p.sh, p.r);
  ctx.clip();
  ctx.translate(p.sx, p.sy);
  ctx.scale(p.sw / CW, p.sh / CH);
  drawCameraFrame(ctx, C);
  ctx.restore();
}

export default {
  id: '2am-crying',
  layouts: { '9x16': { w: 1080, h: 1920 }, '16x9': { w: 1920, h: 1080 } },
  defaultLayout: '9x16',
  fonts: FONTS,
  timeline,
  scenes,
  audioCandidates(audio) {
    const synth = ['../synth/out/2am_crying.wav'];
    const click = ['audio/click.wav'];
    return String(audio.source || '').startsWith('synth') ? [...synth, ...click] : [...click];
  },
  setup(env) {
    env.story = makeStory(env);
  },
  frame(ctx, t, env) {
    const st = env.story;
    const tm = st.loopTime(t);
    const w = st.windowFor(tm);
    const C = makeClip(env, tm, w);
    if (env.layout === '16x9') {
      drawBackdrop(ctx, C);
      drawPhone(ctx, C);
    } else {
      drawCameraFrame(ctx, C);
    }
  },
};

#!/usr/bin/env node
// Render frames in headless Chrome and encode with ffmpeg.
//
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video [options]
//
//   --layout 9x16|16x9      song layout (default: the song's default)
//   --w 540 --h 960         output size (default: half the layout's design size)
//   --fps 30                frame rate
//   --from 0 --to 10        time range in seconds (default: whole song)
//   --out file.mp4          encode an MP4 (libx264 CRF 16 + AAC from the song's audio)
//   --audio file.wav        audio override (default: picked from data/audio.json "source")
//   --workers 8             parallel Chrome instances (frames are independent)
//   --crf 16 --preset medium
//   --sheet file.png        contact sheet: one cell per timeline window (+ song extras)
//   --sheet-mode clips|even --count 24 --cols 10 --cell-w 216
//   --stills 1.5,23,40      single PNG frames  (--stills-dir dir)
//   --check                 determinism check (ordered vs shuffled across workers) and,
//                           if the song loops (outro = intro), frame-exactness of the loop
//
// Chrome: /Applications/Google Chrome.app (override with CHROME=...). Nothing is downloaded.

import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { spawn, execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const opt = (k, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf('--' + k); return i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : d; };
const flag = (k) => argv.includes('--' + k);
const CHROME = process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome';

const songDir = opt('song', 'songs/2am-crying/video');
const layout = opt('layout', null);
const fps = Number(opt('fps', 30));
const workersN = Number(opt('workers', Math.max(2, Math.min(10, Math.floor(os.cpus().length * 0.6)))));
const outMp4 = opt('out', null);
const sheetOut = opt('sheet', null);
const stills = opt('stills', null);
const check = flag('check');

const abs = (p) => (path.isAbsolute(p) ? p : path.join(REPO, p));
const log = (...a) => console.log('[render]', ...a);

function ffprobeDuration(file) {
  try { return Number(execFileSync('ffprobe', ['-v', 'error', '-show_entries', 'format=duration', '-of', 'csv=p=0', file]).toString().trim()); }
  catch { return NaN; }
}

function pickAudio(audioJson) {
  const given = opt('audio', null);
  const dur = audioJson.duration;
  const bar = (60 / audioJson.bpm) * (audioJson.beats_per_bar || 4);
  let file, why;
  if (given) { file = abs(given); why = '--audio'; }
  else if (String(audioJson.source || '').startsWith('synth')) {
    const dir = path.join(abs(songDir), '..', 'synth', 'out');
    const wavs = fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.wav')).map((f) => path.join(dir, f)) : [];
    const scored = wavs.map((f) => ({ f, d: ffprobeDuration(f) })).filter((x) => isFinite(x.d)).sort((a, b) => Math.abs(a.d - dur) - Math.abs(b.d - dur));
    if (scored.length) { file = scored[0].f; why = 'synth (closest duration to audio.json)'; }
  }
  if (!file) { file = path.join(abs(songDir), 'audio', 'click.wav'); why = 'click track (audio.json source: ' + (audioJson.source || '?') + ')'; }
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) throw new Error(`audio ${file} not found (generate the click track: node ${songDir}/tools/make_click.mjs)`);
  const d = ffprobeDuration(file);
  if (Math.abs(d - dur) > bar && !flag('force')) {
    throw new Error(`audio ${path.relative(REPO, file)} is ${d.toFixed(2)}s but data/audio.json says ${dur.toFixed(2)}s (more than a bar apart): the timing and the audio do not match. Regenerate timing or pass --force.`);
  }
  return { file, why, d };
}

async function launchWorker(port, w, h, i) {
  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({
    executablePath: CHROME,
    headless: true,
    args: ['--hide-scrollbars', '--mute-audio', '--force-device-scale-factor=1', '--disable-background-timer-throttling',
      '--disable-renderer-backgrounding', '--disable-backgrounding-occluded-windows', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run'],
    defaultViewport: { width: Math.min(w, 1920), height: Math.min(h, 1920), deviceScaleFactor: 1 },
    protocolTimeout: 600000,
  });
  const page = await browser.newPage();
  const errors = [];
  page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push(String(e)));
  page.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' || m.type() === 'warning') errors.push(m.text()); });
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ render: '1', w: String(w), h: String(h), fps: String(fps) });
  if (layout) q.set('layout', layout);
  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${songDir}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load', timeout: 120000 });
  await page.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.error)', { timeout: 120000 });
  const info = await page.evaluate(() => (window.__render.error ? { error: window.__render.error } : {
    duration: window.__render.duration, windows: window.__render.windows, w: window.__render.w, h: window.__render.h, layout: window.__render.layout,
  }));
  if (info.error) throw new Error(`worker ${i}: page failed: ${info.error}\n${errors.join('\n')}`);
  if (i === 0 && errors.length) log('page messages:\n  ' + errors.join('\n  '));
  return { browser, page, info, errors };
}

async function main() {
  const audioJson = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(abs(songDir), 'data', 'audio.json'), 'utf8'));
  const server = createServer(REPO);
  const port = await listen(server, 0);
  // output size from the layout's design size unless given
  let w = Number(opt('w', 0)), h = Number(opt('h', 0));
  if (!w || !h) {
    const portrait = (layout || '9x16') === '9x16';
    w = w || (portrait ? 540 : 960); h = h || (portrait ? 960 : 540);
  }
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const nW = outMp4 ? workersN : 1;
  const workers = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: nW }, (_, i) => launchWorker(port, w, h, i)));
  const info = workers[0].info;
  log(`${workers.length} worker(s) ready in ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s · ${info.w}x${info.h} @${fps} · layout ${info.layout} · song ${info.duration.toFixed(2)}s · ${info.windows.length} windows`);
  const P = workers[0].page;

  try {
    if (stills) {
      const dir = abs(opt('stills-dir', path.join(songDir, 'renders', 'stills')));
      fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
      for (const s of stills.split(',').map(Number)) {
        const url = await P.evaluate((t) => window.__render.png(t), s);
        const f = path.join(dir, `still-${info.layout}-${s.toFixed(3)}.png`);
        fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
        log('still', path.relative(REPO, f));
      }
    }

    if (sheetOut) {
      const mode = opt('sheet-mode', 'clips');
      let cells;
      if (mode === 'even') {
        const n = Number(opt('count', 24));
        cells = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ t: (info.duration * (i + 0.5)) / n, label: `#${i + 1}` }));
      } else cells = await P.evaluate(() => window.__render.heroCells());
      const portrait = info.h > info.w;
      const cols = Number(opt('cols', portrait ? 10 : 5));
      const cellW = Number(opt('cell-w', portrait ? 216 : 384));
      const title = opt('title', `${songDir} · ${info.layout} · ${cells.length} cells · ${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 16)}`);
      const url = await P.evaluate((c, o) => window.__render.sheet(c, o), cells, { cols, cellW, title });
      const f = abs(sheetOut);
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(f), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(f, Buffer.from(url.split(',')[1], 'base64'));
      log(`sheet (${cells.length} cells) -> ${path.relative(REPO, f)}`);
    }

    if (check) await runChecks(workers, info);

    if (outMp4) await encode(workers, info, w, h, audioJson);
  } finally {
    await Promise.all(workers.map((x) => x.browser.close().catch(() => {})));
    server.close();
  }
}

async function runChecks(workers, info) {
  const P = workers[0].page;
  const ts = [0.5, info.duration * 0.21, info.duration * 0.37, info.duration * 0.55, info.duration * 0.71, info.duration * 0.88].map((t) => Math.round(t * fps) / fps);
  const ordered = [];
  for (const t of ts) ordered.push(await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), t));
  const shuffled = [...ts].map((t, i) => ({ t, i })).sort((a, b) => ((a.i * 7919) % 13) - ((b.i * 7919) % 13));
  // on a fresh worker (or the same one, out of order) with unrelated frames in between
  const Q = workers[workers.length > 1 ? 1 : 0].page;
  const hashes = {};
  for (const { t, i } of shuffled) { await Q.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), info.duration - t); hashes[i] = await Q.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), t); }
  const bad = ts.filter((_, i) => hashes[i] !== ordered[i]);
  log(bad.length ? `DETERMINISM FAIL at ${bad.join(', ')}` : `determinism ok (${ts.length} frames, ordered vs shuffled)`);
  const intro = info.windows[0], outro = info.windows.find((x) => x.plate === intro.plate && x.params.loop);
  if (outro) {
    const outroStartFrame = Math.ceil(outro.start * fps - 1e-6);
    let fails = 0;
    for (const j of [3, 17, 40, 60, 90]) {
      const k = outroStartFrame + j;
      if (k / fps >= info.duration) continue;
      const jj = Math.round(k - outro.start * fps); // the intro frame the loop maps onto
      const a = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), jj / fps);
      const b = await P.evaluate((x) => window.__render.hash(x), k / fps);
      if (a !== b) { fails++; log(`LOOP MISMATCH: outro frame ${k} vs intro frame ${jj}`); }
    }
    log(fails ? `loop check: ${fails} mismatches` : 'loop ok (outro frames are intro frames)');
  }
}

async function encode(workers, info, w, h, audioJson) {
  const from = Number(opt('from', 0));
  const to = Math.min(Number(opt('to', info.duration)), info.duration);
  const f0 = Math.round(from * fps), f1 = Math.round(to * fps);
  const N = f1 - f0;
  const audio = pickAudio(audioJson);
  log(`audio: ${path.relative(REPO, audio.file)} (${audio.why}, ${audio.d.toFixed(2)}s)`);
  const out = abs(outMp4);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(out), { recursive: true });
  const ff = spawn('ffmpeg', [
    '-y', '-loglevel', 'error', '-stats_period', '5',
    '-f', 'rawvideo', '-pix_fmt', 'rgba', '-s', `${w}x${h}`, '-r', String(fps), '-i', 'pipe:0',
    '-ss', String(from), '-t', String(N / fps), '-i', audio.file,
    '-map', '0:v', '-map', '1:a',
    '-vf', 'scale=out_color_matrix=bt709:out_range=tv,format=yuv420p',
    '-c:v', 'libx264', '-preset', opt('preset', 'medium'), '-crf', String(opt('crf', 16)),
    '-colorspace', 'bt709', '-color_primaries', 'bt709', '-color_trc', 'bt709', '-color_range', 'tv',
    '-c:a', 'aac', '-b:a', '192k', '-shortest', '-movflags', '+faststart', out,
  ], { stdio: ['pipe', 'inherit', 'inherit'] });
  const ffDone = new Promise((res, rej) => ff.on('close', (c) => (c === 0 ? res() : rej(new Error('ffmpeg exited ' + c)))));

  const buffered = new Map();
  let next = f0, dispatched = f0, written = 0;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  let paintMs = 0;
  const BATCH = 4;
  const maxAhead = workers.length * BATCH * 3;
  async function flush() {
    while (buffered.has(next)) {
      const buf = buffered.get(next);
      buffered.delete(next);
      next++; written++;
      if (!ff.stdin.write(buf)) await new Promise((r) => ff.stdin.once('drain', r));
    }
  }
  let lastLog = 0;
  async function work(wk) {
    while (dispatched < f1) {
      while (dispatched - next > maxAhead) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5));
      const a = dispatched;
      const b = Math.min(f1, a + BATCH);
      dispatched = b;
      const ts = [];
      for (let k = a; k < b; k++) ts.push(k / fps);
      const s0 = Date.now();
      const out64 = await wk.page.evaluate((x) => window.__render.frames(x), ts);
      paintMs += Date.now() - s0;
      out64.forEach((b64, i) => buffered.set(a + i, Buffer.from(b64, 'base64')));
      await flush();
      if (Date.now() - lastLog > 5000) {
        lastLog = Date.now();
        const el = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
        log(`${written}/${N} frames · ${(written / el).toFixed(1)} fps · eta ${((N - written) / Math.max(1e-3, written / el)).toFixed(0)}s`);
      }
    }
  }
  await Promise.all(workers.map(work));
  await flush();
  ff.stdin.end();
  await ffDone;
  const el = (Date.now() - t0) / 1000;
  log(`wrote ${path.relative(REPO, out)}: ${N} frames in ${el.toFixed(1)}s · wall ${((el * 1000) / N).toFixed(1)} ms/frame · per-worker paint+transfer ${(paintMs / N).toFixed(1)} ms/frame · ${workers.length} workers`);
}

main().catch((e) => { console.error('[render] FAILED:', e.message || e); process.exit(1); });

import puppeteer from '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/node_modules/puppeteer-core/lib/esm/puppeteer/puppeteer-core.js';

#!/usr/bin/env node
// Smoke test for the preview page: loads index.html in headless Chrome with each given
// query, waits for the preview to start, reports errors and saves a screenshot.
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --q "t=23" --q "layout=16x9&t=40" [--shots dir]
import path from 'node:path';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const song = argv.includes('--song') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--song') + 1] : 'songs/2am-crying/video';
const qs = argv.flatMap((a, i) => (a === '--q' ? [argv[i + 1]] : []));
const shots = argv.includes('--shots') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--shots') + 1] : null;
const srv = createServer(REPO);
const port = await listen(srv, 0);
const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true, defaultViewport: { width: 1280, height: 1000 } });
let bad = 0;
for (const q of qs.length ? qs : ['t=0']) {
  const p = await b.newPage();
  const errs = [];
  p.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(String(e)));
  p.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/favicon|404/.test(m.text())) errs.push(m.text()); });
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${song}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await p.waitForFunction('window.__preview || (window.__render && window.__render.error)', { timeout: 30000 });
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));
  const info = await p.evaluate(() => ({ err: document.getElementById('pv-err')?.textContent || '', bar: [...document.querySelectorAll('.pv-bar span')].map((s) => s.textContent).join(' | ') }));
  if (info.err || errs.length) bad++;
  console.log(`[smoke] ${q}: ${info.bar}${info.err ? '\n  RENDER ERROR: ' + info.err : ''}${errs.length ? '\n  ' + errs.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  if (shots) { fs.mkdirSync(shots, { recursive: true }); await p.screenshot({ path: path.join(shots, `preview-${q.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '_')}.png`) }); }
  await p.close();
}
await b.close();
srv.close();
process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);

file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/scenes/nap.js
// Naps. mode 'tip' (11am): hard cut to daylight, eyes closed, the phone slowly tipping
// over until it looks up at the ceiling fan. mode 'carseat' (3pm): head at an angle in the
// car seat. mode 'long' (the bridge, one long take): bottle, eyes getting heavy, the phone
// slipping a degree each beat, then tipping flat onto the ceiling fan, whose blades slow
// with the half-time.
import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';
import { ceiling, cribBack, fillAll, bottle, carSeat, carSeatStraps, windowPane } from '../props.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, babyLight, wordT, CW, CH } from './common.js';
import { lerp, TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

const UP = 1700; // how far above the baby the ceiling fan sits in the strip

/** Fan angle with speed ramping v0 -> v1 over [a, b] (analytic, so frames stay independent). */
function fanAngle(t, a, b, v0, v1) {
  if (t <= a) return v0 * t;
  const base = v0 * a;
  if (t <= b) { const d = t - a; return base + v0 * d + ((v1 - v0) * d * d) / (2 * (b - a)); }
  return base + v0 * (b - a) + ((v1 - v0) * (b - a)) / 2 + v1 * (t - b);
}

function lyingBaby(ctx, C, o = {}) {
  // the baby on its back on the crib sheet, seen from the phone held above its face
  const { G } = C;
  fillAll(ctx, G.sheet);
  // sheet folds
  ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(G.sheetShade, 0.55);
  ctx.lineWidth = 10;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-200 + i * 260, 300); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-60 + i * 260, 900, -220 + i * 260, 1900); ctx.stroke(); }
  // crib rail along the top of the frame, between the face and the ceiling
  ctx.fillStyle = G.crib;
  ctx.fillRect(-900, 70, CW + 1800, 80);
  for (let x = -600; x < CW + 600; x += 110) ctx.fillRect(x, -420, 40, 520);
  drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient'), outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'line', ...o });
}

export default {
  camera(C) {
    const p = C.params;
    if (p.mode === 'carseat') return { shake: 0.6, kick: 0.4, tilt: 0.04 * Math.sin(C.t * 7) * 0.2 };
    if (p.mode === 'tip') {
      const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.18, C.end + C.dur * 0.15));
      return { dy: UP * 0.92 * k, tilt: 0.42 * ease.inQuad(k), zoom: 1 - 0.08 * k, shake: 0.4, kick: 0.25 };
    }
    // long take: a degree per beat, then the tip-over at "11am"
    const elev = C.story.clauses.find((c) => c.section === 'bridge' && /11am/.test(c.n));
    const tipA = elev ? C.B.snap(elev.start, 'downbeat') : C.start + C.dur * 0.7;
    const tipB = Math.min(C.end - C.B.barDur * 0.5, tipA + C.B.barDur * 2);
    const beats = Math.max(0, C.B.beatIndex(Math.min(C.t, tipA)) - C.B.beatIndex(C.start));
    const slip = Math.floor(beats) + ease.outCubic(beats % 1);
    const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, tipA, tipB));
    return { dy: UP * 0.92 * k, tilt: 0.022 * slip * (1 - k) + 0.12 * k, zoom: 1 - 0.06 * k, shake: 0.35, kick: 0 };
  },
  caption(C) {
    return C.params.mode === 'long' ? { family: '"Inter", sans-serif' } : {};
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const p = C.params;
    const { G } = C;
    if (p.mode === 'carseat') {
      // car window behind, daylight, the seat shell around the head
      fillAll(ctx, mix(G.seatShade, '#000000', 0.2));
      windowPane(ctx, G, { x: -80, y: 120, w: 1240, h: 640, curtains: false });
      const tilt = 0.38;
      carSeat(ctx, G, { x: SELFIE.x, y: SELFIE.y, r: SELFIE.r, tilt });
      const x = SELFIE.x + 60, y = SELFIE.y + 30;
      drawBaby(ctx, { x, y, r: SELFIE.r, C: babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient'), outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.25, tilt });
      carSeatStraps(ctx, G, { x, y, r: SELFIE.r });
      return;
    }
    if (p.mode === 'tip') {
      lyingBaby(ctx, C);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(0, -UP);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, 900 + 520 + 60); ctx.clip();
      ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 540, y: 300, R: 520, angle: C.t * 3.2, blur: 0.7 });
      ctx.restore();
      return;
    }
    // ---- the long take -----------------------------------------------------------
    const cl = C.story.clauses.filter((c) => c.section === 'bridge');
    const at = (re, f) => (cl.find((c) => re.test(c.n)) || {}).start ?? f;
    const tWarm = at(/10:55/, C.start + C.dur * 0.25);
    const tHeavy = at(/10:58/, C.start + C.dur * 0.5);
    const tNap = at(/11am/, C.start + C.dur * 0.7);
    const heavy = wordT(C, 'heavy', tWarm + 1.2);
    const open = 1 - 0.45 * ease.inOutQuad(seg(C.t, tWarm, heavy + 0.4)) - 0.55 * ease.inOutQuad(seg(C.t, tHeavy, tNap));
    const blink = C.t < tHeavy ? 0 : 0.35 * Math.max(0, Math.sin((C.t - tHeavy) * 5)) * (1 - seg(C.t, tHeavy, tNap));
    const droop = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, tHeavy, tNap + 0.6));
    lyingBaby(ctx, C, {
      eyes: 'open', open: clamp(open - blink), look: [0, -0.3 * (1 - droop)], mouth: droop > 0.6 ? 'o' : 'none', mouthOpen: 0.25,
      free: droop < 0.85 ? { mode: 'hold', at: [lerp(0.35, 0.95, droop), lerp(1.15, 1.9, droop)] } : { mode: 'down' },
    });
    // the bottle: at the mouth, then sliding off to the side as the hands go heavy
    if (droop < 0.95) {
      bottle(ctx, G, {
        x: SELFIE.x + lerp(30, 300, droop), y: SELFIE.y + lerp(230, 520, droop), a: lerp(-2.6, -1.7, droop), s: 1.05,
        level: lerp(0.6, 0.15, seg(C.t, C.start, tHeavy)),
      });
    }
    // warmth: a soft sun patch drifting over the sheet
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(300, 700, 50, 300, 700, 900);
    g.addColorStop(0, rgba('#fff3c4', 0.28));
    g.addColorStop(1, rgba('#fff3c4', 0));
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
    // the ceiling above, with the fan slowing down
    const v0 = 9.0, v1 = 1.4;
    const ang = fanAngle(C.t - C.start, tHeavy - C.start, C.end - C.start, v0, v1);
    const speed = lerp(v0, v1, seg(C.t, tHeavy, C.end));
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(0, -UP);
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, 900 + 520 + 60); ctx.clip();
    ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 540, y: 300, R: 520, angle: ang, blur: clamp(speed / 8) });
    ctx.restore();
  },
};
result
File created successfully at: /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/scenes/nap.js (file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)
Writesongs/2am-crying/video/scenes/pooping.js
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/scenes/pooping.js
// 1pm pooping: concentration face. Caption only. Held absolutely still.
// 2pm pooping again: the identical frame (same light, same face, no motion); the caption
// gets "again" slammed on in red.
import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';
import { wall, windowPane } from '../props.js';
import { grade } from '../tod.js';
import { SELFIE, babyLight } from './common.js';

const FROZEN_MIN = 13 * 60; // both clips use the 1pm light so the frames are identical

export default {
  camera() { return { still: true, grain: 0.6 }; },
  caption(C) {
    return C.params.again ? { sticker: { word: 'again', color: '#ffffff', pill: '#ff2a2a', size: 1.75, rot: -0.12 } } : {};
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const G = grade(FROZEN_MIN);
    const C1 = { ...C, G };
    wall(ctx, G);

old="""const cache = new Map();
export function grade(min, base = BASE) {
  const key = Math.floor(((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440);
  let G = cache.get(key);
  if (G) return G;
  const L = light(key);"""
new="""const cache = new Map();
/** Lit palette for a minute. soft < 1 pulls the light toward neutral (used for the baby,
 *  so faces stay readable while the room carries the colour temperature). */
export function grade(min, base = BASE, soft = 1) {
  const key = Math.floor(((min % 1440) + 1440) % 1440);
  const ck = soft === 1 && base === BASE ? key : `${key}:${soft}:${base === BASE ? 'b' : 'x'}`;
  let G = cache.get(ck);
  if (G) return G;
  const L0 = light(key);
  const L = soft === 1 ? L0 : { ...L0, mul: L0.mul.map((m) => 1 + (m - 1) * soft), lift: L0.lift.map((l) => l * soft) };"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old="""  G.ink = mix(G.ink, base.ink, 0.6);
  cache.set(key, G);"""
new="""  G.ink = mix(G.ink, base.ink, 0.6);
  G.light = L0;
  cache.set(ck, G);"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)

p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs'
s=open(p).read()

# 1. return lyric lines/adlibs/sfx from the synth reader
old="""  return { bpm, sections: PLAN.map((p) => ({ name: p.name, start: mapped[p.name].start, end: mapped[p.name].end })), beats, kicks, duration };"""
new="""  const synthSecName = Object.fromEntries(PLAN.map((p) => [mapped[p.name].src, p.name]));
  const sung = Array.isArray(j.lyric_lines) ? j.lyric_lines.map((l) => ({ ...l, plan: synthSecName[l.section] ?? null })) : null;
  const adlibs = Array.isArray(j.adlibs) ? j.adlibs.map((a) => ({ ...a, plan: synthSecName[a.section] ?? null })) : [];
  return { bpm, sections: PLAN.map((p) => ({ name: p.name, start: mapped[p.name].start, end: mapped[p.name].end })), beats, kicks, duration, sung, adlibs, sfx: j.sfx || [] };"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)

# 2. helper: align sheet words to synth syllables
old="""// ------------------------------------------------------------------ build ----"""
new="""// ------------------------------------------------- synth sung-line alignment ----
const lettersOf = (w) => String(w).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, '');

/**
 * Time a clause's sheet words from a synth lyric line ("it's TWO a M and I'm CRY-ing" +
 * syllable onsets). Words map 1:1 to synth tokens, except the time word ("2am", "10:55"),
 * which takes synth tokens until the next sheet word matches. Returns null on mismatch.
 */
function alignToSung(words, sl) {
  if (!sl || !Array.isArray(sl.syllables) || !sl.syllables.length) return null;
  const toks = String(sl.text).split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  let si = 0;
  const tokSyl = toks.map((tk) => {
    const n = tk.split('-').filter(Boolean).length || 1;
    const syl = sl.syllables.slice(si, si + n);
    si += n;
    return { tk, syl };
  });
  if (si !== sl.syllables.length) return null;
  const out = [];
  let ti = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < words.length; k++) {
    const w = words[k];
    if (/\\d/.test(w)) {
      const nextL = k + 1 < words.length ? lettersOf(words[k + 1]) : null;
      const group = [];
      while (ti < tokSyl.length && (nextL == null || lettersOf(tokSyl[ti].tk) !== nextL || !group.length)) group.push(tokSyl[ti++]);
      if (!group.length) return null;
      const syl = group.flatMap((g) => g.syl);
      out.push({ w, start: syl[0].onset_s, end: syl.at(-1).onset_s + syl.at(-1).duration_s });
    } else {
      const g = tokSyl[ti++];
      if (!g || lettersOf(g.tk) !== lettersOf(w)) return null;
      out.push({ w, start: g.syl[0].onset_s, end: g.syl.at(-1).onset_s + g.syl.at(-1).duration_s });
    }
  }
  return ti === tokSyl.length ? out : null;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ build ----"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)

# 3. use it in the per-line loop when the synth has sung lines for this section
old="""    let cursor = b0;
    sheetSec.lines.forEach((text, li) => {"""

    // synth sung lines for this section (clause-level, in order), and its ad-libs
    const sungQ = useSynth && synth.sung ? synth.sung.filter((l) => l.plan === sec.name) : null;
    const adlibQ = useSynth ? (synth.adlibs || []).filter((a) => a.plan === sec.name) : [];
    const sheetUnits = sheetSec.lines.flatMap((text) => (/^\\(/.test(text) ? [text] : clauses(text.split(/\\s+/).filter(Boolean)).map((c) => c.join(' '))));
    const sungOK = sungQ && sungQ.length === sheetUnits.length;
    if (sungQ && !sungOK) console.log(`[mock_timing] ${sec.name}: synth has ${sungQ.length} sung lines, sheet has ${sheetUnits.length} units; spreading words evenly`);
    let unitIdx = 0;
    sheetSec.lines.forEach((text, li) => {"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)

old="""      words = words.map((w) => ({ w: w.w, start: r6(w.start), end: r6(w.end), conf: 1.0 }));
      lines.push({ i: lines.length, text, start: words[0].start, end: words.at(-1).end, section: sec.name, words });"""
new="""      // Prefer the synth's sung syllables when its lines line up with the sheet.
      if (sungOK) {
        const units = /^\\(/.test(text) ? [tokens] : clauses(tokens);
        const timed = [];
        let ok = true;
        for (const u of units) {
          const sl = sungQ[unitIdx++];
          const main = u.filter((w) => !/^\\(.*\\)$/.test(w) && !/^\\(/.test(w));
          const back = u.filter((w) => /^\\(/.test(w) || /\\)$/.test(w));
          let tw = alignToSung(/^\\(/.test(text) ? u.map((w) => w.replace(/[()]/g, '')) : main, sl);
          if (tw && /^\\(/.test(text)) tw = tw.map((x, k) => ({ ...x, w: u[k] }));
          if (!tw) {
            if (sl && !(sl.syllables || []).length && !/^\\((untz|click)/i.test(text)) {
              // spoken: spread across the synth line's span
              const a = sl.start_s, b = a + (sl.end_s - sl.start_s) * FILL;
              tw = u.map((w, k) => ({ w, start: a + ((b - a) * k) / u.length, end: a + ((b - a) * (k + 1)) / u.length }));
            } else { ok = false; break; }
          } else if (back.length && !/^\\(/.test(text)) {
            const ad = adlibQ.shift();
            if (ad) {
              const end = (ad.tail_hit_s ?? ad.hits_s.at(-1)) + 0.15;
              back.forEach((w, k) => tw.push({ w, start: ad.hits_s[0] + ((end - ad.hits_s[0]) * k) / back.length, end: ad.hits_s[0] + ((end - ad.hits_s[0]) * (k + 1)) / back.length }));
            } else ok = false;
          }
          timed.push(...tw);
        }
        if (ok && timed.length === tokens.length) words = timed;
        else if (!/^\\((untz|click)/i.test(text)) console.log(`[mock_timing] ${sec.name}: could not align "${text}" to the synth; spreading evenly`);
      }
      words = words.map((w) => ({ w: w.w, start: r6(w.start), end: r6(w.end), conf: 1.0 }));
      lines.push({ i: lines.length, text, start: words[0].start, end: words.at(-1).end, section: sec.name, words });"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace("""    notes: `MOCK timing (tools/mock_timing.mjs, lyric sheet ${path.basename(LYRICS_TXT)}, grid source: ${source}). ` +

      (useSynth ? 'Word times come from the synth demo\\'s sung syllables (song.json lyric_lines/adlibs) where they line up; ' : 'Clauses share each line\\'s voiced span; words spread evenly; ') +
      'replace with the shared/align output once the Suno vocal exists.',""")
open(p,'w').write(s)

python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs'
s=open(p).read()
old="""  let si = 0;
  const tokSyl = toks.map((tk) => {
    const n = tk.split('-').filter(Boolean).length || 1;
    const syl = sl.syllables.slice(si, si + n);
    si += n;
    return { tk, syl };
  });
  if (si !== sl.syllables.length) return null;"""
new="""  // each token takes syllables until their letters spell it ("napping" = NAP + ping)
  let si = 0;
  const tokSyl = [];
  for (const tk of toks) {
    const want = lettersOf(tk);
    const syl = [];
    let got = '';
    while (si < sl.syllables.length && (got.length < want.length || !syl.length)) {
      syl.push(sl.syllables[si]);
      got += lettersOf(sl.syllables[si++].syllable);
    }
    if (got !== want) return null;
    tokSyl.push({ tk, syl });
  }
  if (si !== sl.syllables.length) return null;"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

// Standing in the crib, a little further from the lens.
//   up (5:45): flash, standing, grinning; the parents' alarm clock behind reads 5:45
//   stand (6am) / stand + timer (6:15): the same frame twice, the second with a stopwatch
//   wearing (7:30): the milk-stained onesie, proud, chin up
//   run (7:15pm): naked, towel cape, motion blur, frame shaking, laughing
import { drawBaby, babyColors } from '../baby.js';
import { wall, windowPane, cribBack, nightstand, alarmClock, timerBadge, fillAll, doorway } from '../props.js';
import { FLASH } from '../tod.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, babyLight, flashPop, CW, CH } from './common.js';
import { hash } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

const STAND = { x: 540, y: 1060, r: 250 };

export default {
  camera(C) {
    const p = C.params;
    if (p.mode === 'run') return { shake: 4.5, kick: 2, tilt: 0.12 * Math.sin(C.t * 11) };
    if (p.mode === 'wearing') return { tilt: -0.05 };
    return {};
  },
  overlay(ctx, C) {
    if (C.params.timer) timerBadge(ctx, 15 * 60 + C.lt, { x: 540, y: 1560 });
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const p = C.params;
    const { G } = C;
    if (p.mode === 'run') {
      // a hallway smearing past
      wall(ctx, G, { glow: 0.1 });
      const off = (C.t * 2600) % 700;
      for (let i = -2; i < 5; i++) {
        const x = i * 700 - off;
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.trim, 0.8);
        ctx.fillRect(x, 200, 60, 1300);
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.doorDark, 0.35);
        ctx.fillRect(x + 120, 300, 300, 1000);
      }
      ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.floor, 1);
      ctx.fillRect(-900, 1500, CW + 1800, 1400);
      for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.12);
        ctx.fillRect(hash(i, 3) * CW, hash(i, 4) * CH, 260 + 300 * hash(i, 5), 6);
      }
      const L = babyLight(C, 'naked');
      for (let gI = 2; gI >= 0; gI--) {
        ctx.globalAlpha = gI === 0 ? 1 : 0.22;
        drawBaby(ctx, { ...STAND, x: STAND.x + gI * 70, C: { ...L, towel: G.towel }, outfit: 'towel', eyes: 'closed', mouth: 'grin', mouthOpen: 0.9, free: { mode: 'up', angle: C.t * 14 }, tilt: 0.12 });
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      return;
    }
    // the parents' room behind the crib
    const night = G.light.night;
    wall(ctx, G, { glow: 0.12 });
    windowPane(ctx, G, { x: 70, y: 220, w: 300, h: 430, blinds: 0.6 });
    nightstand(ctx, G, { x: 650, y: 860, w: 420, h: 500 });
    alarmClock(ctx, G, { x: 860, y: 790, text: p.mode === 'up' ? '5:45' : (p.timer ? '6:15' : (p.mode === 'wearing' ? '7:30' : '6:00')), s: 1.1, glow: mix('#ff3b30', '#ff8a7a', 0.2) });
    cribBack(ctx, G, { railY: 1030, bottomY: 1780, gap: 100, slatW: 30 });
    const flash = p.mode === 'up';
    const L = flash ? babyColors(FLASH, 'onesie') : babyLight(C, 'onesie', 'ambient');
    drawBaby(ctx, {
      ...STAND, C: L, outfit: 'onesie',
      eyes: p.mode === 'up' ? 'wide' : 'open', mouth: p.mode === 'up' ? 'grin' : p.mode === 'wearing' ? 'smile' : 'line', mouthOpen: 0.8,
      stain: p.mode === 'wearing', tilt: p.mode === 'wearing' ? -0.08 : 0, look: p.mode === 'wearing' ? [0, -0.4] : [0, 0],
      free: { mode: 'hold', at: [0.95, 2.3] }, flash: flash ? 0.55 : 0,
    });
    // the front of the crib rail, gripped
    ctx.fillStyle = G.crib;
    ctx.fillRect(-900, STAND.y + 2.15 * STAND.r, CW + 1800, 70);
    if (flash) {
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(STAND.x, STAND.y, STAND.r, STAND.x, STAND.y, STAND.r * 4.2);
      g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(10,12,22,0)');
      g.addColorStop(1, `rgba(10,12,22,${0.35 + 0.3 * night})`);
      ctx.fillStyle = g;
      ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
      flashPop(ctx, C.lt, 0.55);
    }
  },
};

Writesongs/2am-crying/video/scenes/highchair.js
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/scenes/highchair.js
// High chair. spoon (12pm crying, a spoon mid-air), eat (8am / 6pm), throw (8:05: food
// arcing past the lens, then a splat on the glass), floor (6:05: the camera tilts down to
// the food on the floor; the caption stays deadpan).
import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';
import { wall, windowPane, chairBack, tray, spoon, hand, foodBlob, lensSplat, fillAll } from '../props.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, babyLight, CW, CH } from './common.js';
import { hash, lerp } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { mix } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

const SPLAT_AT = 0.42; // fraction of the throw clip when the food hits the lens

function kitchen(ctx, C) {
  const { G } = C;
  wall(ctx, G, { glow: 0.2 });
  windowPane(ctx, G, { x: 640, y: 200, w: 360, h: 460, curtains: false, blinds: 0.25 });
  chairBack(ctx, G);
}

function trayFood(ctx, G, seed = 0) {
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) foodBlob(ctx, i % 2 ? G.food : G.food2, 180 + hash(i, 30 + seed) * 720, 1730 + hash(i, 31 + seed) * 120, 26 + 18 * hash(i, 32 + seed), i + seed);
}

export default {
  camera(C) {
    const p = C.params;
    if (p.mode === 'floor') {
      const k = ease.inOutCubic(seg(C.t, C.start, C.start + Math.min(0.6, C.dur * 0.5)));
      return { dy: -1250 * k, tilt: 0.1 * k };
    }
    if (p.mode === 'throw') return { shake: C.lt > C.dur * SPLAT_AT ? 2.2 : 1 };
    return {};
  },
  overlay(ctx, C) {
    if (C.params.mode !== 'throw') return;
    const k = seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * SPLAT_AT, C.start + C.dur * SPLAT_AT + 0.08);
    if (k > 0) lensSplat(ctx, C.G.food, 640, 980, 210, ease.outBack(k, 2), 5);
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const p = C.params;
    const { G } = C;
    const L = babyLight(C, 'onesie');
    if (p.mode === 'floor') {
      // the floor below the tray (the camera tilts down to it)
      kitchen(ctx, C);
      ctx.fillStyle = G.floor;
      ctx.fillRect(-900, 1900, CW + 1800, 2400);
      ctx.strokeStyle = rgba(mix(G.floor, '#000000', 0.25), 0.6);
      ctx.lineWidth = 8;
      for (let y = 2000; y < 4300; y += 170) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-900, y); ctx.lineTo(CW + 900, y); ctx.stroke(); }
      for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) foodBlob(ctx, i % 3 ? G.food : G.food2, 80 + hash(i, 50) * 920, 2650 + hash(i, 51) * 900, 30 + 34 * hash(i, 52), i);
      // the chair's legs
      ctx.fillStyle = G.chairShade;
      ctx.fillRect(160, 1900, 60, 2200); ctx.fillRect(860, 1900, 60, 2200);
      drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: L, outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'open', look: [0, 1], mouth: 'line', free: { mode: 'down' } });
      tray(ctx, G, { y: 1640 });
      return;
    }
    kitchen(ctx, C);
    const o = { ...SELFIE, C: L, outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'open', mouth: 'line', free: { mode: 'down' } };
    if (p.mode === 'spoon') { o.eyes = 'squeeze'; o.mouth = 'o'; o.mouthOpen = 0.9; o.tears = (C.lt * 0.8) % 1; o.blush = 0.4; }
    if (p.mode === 'eat') { o.mouth = 'o'; o.mouthOpen = 0.35 + 0.3 * Math.abs(Math.sin(C.t * 8)); o.free = { mode: 'hold', at: [0.35, 0.75] }; }
    if (p.mode === 'throw') {
      const wind = seg(C.t, C.start, C.start + C.dur * 0.15);
      o.free = { mode: 'up', angle: lerp(0, 2.2, wind) };
      o.mouth = C.lt > C.dur * SPLAT_AT ? 'grin' : 'o'; o.mouthOpen = 0.6;
    }
    drawBaby(ctx, o);
    tray(ctx, G, { y: 1640 });
    trayFood(ctx, G, p.mode === 'eat' ? 0 : 3);
    if (p.mode === 'eat') foodBlob(ctx, G.food, SELFIE.x + 0.35 * SELFIE.r, SELFIE.y + 0.62 * SELFIE.r, 34, 9);
    if (p.mode === 'spoon') {
      // an adult hand from the right, the spoon hovering, waiting
      const hover = Math.sin(C.t * 5) * 14;
      hand(ctx, G, { x: 1010, y: 1260 + hover, a: 0.25, s: 1 });
      spoon(ctx, G, { x: 780, y: 1240 + hover, a: 0.25 });
    }
    if (p.mode === 'throw') {
      // the food arcs out of the hand toward the lens, growing as it comes
      const k = seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.12, C.start + C.dur * SPLAT_AT);
      if (k > 0 && k < 1) {
        const x = lerp(SELFIE.x + 1.3 * SELFIE.r, 640, k);
        const y = lerp(SELFIE.y - 0.2 * SELFIE.r, 980, k) - Math.sin(Math.PI * k) * 380;
        foodBlob(ctx, G.food, x, y, lerp(40, 260, ease.inCubic(k)), 7);
      }
    }
  },
};

// The ceiling fan. point (10:30): the baby points up, then the camera whips up to the fan.
// cry (5:30): the fan again, accusingly; the crying baby's chin at the bottom of frame.
import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';
import { wall, windowPane, ceiling } from '../props.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, babyLight, CW, CH } from './common.js';

const UP = 2000;

export default {
  camera(C) {
    if (C.params.mode === 'point') {
      const k = ease.inOutExpo(seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.38, C.start + C.dur * 0.62));
      return { dy: UP * k, tilt: -0.06 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k), shake: 1 + 3 * Math.sin(Math.PI * k) };
    }
    return { tilt: 0.08, shake: 1.6 };
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const p = C.params;
    const { G } = C;
    const L = babyLight(C, 'onesie');
    if (p.mode === 'point') {
      wall(ctx, G);
      windowPane(ctx, G, { x: 60, y: 260, w: 330, h: 470 });
      drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: L, outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'open', look: [0.2, -1], mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 0.4, free: { mode: 'up', angle: 0.5 } });
      // the ceiling above the wall
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(0, -UP);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 900 + UP - CH); ctx.clip();
      ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 540, y: 950, R: 540, angle: C.t * 4.5, blur: 0.8 });
      ctx.restore();
      // the whip itself: a vertical smear at peak speed
      const k = seg(C.t, C.start + C.dur * 0.38, C.start + C.dur * 0.62);
      const sm = Math.sin(Math.PI * k);
      if (sm > 0.05) {
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba(G.wall, 0.55 * sm);
        ctx.fillRect(-900, -UP - 900, CW + 1800, UP + CH + 1800);
      }
      return;
    }
    // accusing: the fan up top, the crying chin and mouth at the bottom edge
    ceiling(ctx, G, { x: 560, y: 760, R: 560, angle: C.t * 3.0, blur: 0.6 });
    drawBaby(ctx, {
      x: 540, y: 2060, r: 420, C: L, outfit: 'onesie', eyes: 'squeeze', mouth: 'o', mouthOpen: 1, look: [0, -1], tears: (C.lt * 0.8) % 1, blush: 0.5,
      free: { mode: 'up', angle: 0.2 },
    });
  },
};

// 7pm splashing: tiles, the waterline across the chest, a hand slapping the water on the
// beat, droplets landing on the lens; the caption drips.
import { drawBaby } from '../baby.js';
import { bathTiles, waterSurface, droplets } from '../props.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, SELFIE, babyLight, clipBeat, CW, CH } from './common.js';
import { hash, TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';

export default {
  camera() { return { shake: 1.8 }; },
  caption(C) { return { drip: clamp(C.lt / C.dur) }; },
  overlay(ctx, C) {
    // droplets accumulate: a new batch every beat
    const cb = clipBeat(C);
    for (let b = 0; b <= cb.i; b++) droplets(ctx, 5, 400 + b * 17, C.t - (C.start + b * C.B.beatDur), { alpha: 1 });
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const { G } = C;
    bathTiles(ctx, G);
    const cb = clipBeat(C);
    const slap = Math.exp(-cb.phase * 4);
    drawBaby(ctx, { ...SELFIE, C: babyLight(C, 'naked'), outfit: 'naked', eyes: cb.phase < 0.2 ? 'squeeze' : 'open', mouth: 'grin', mouthOpen: 0.7, free: { mode: 'up', angle: cb.phase * 3 } });
    waterSurface(ctx, G, SELFIE.y + 1.6 * SELFIE.r, C.t);
    // splash spray on each beat
    ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.85 * slap);
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
      const a = -Math.PI * (0.15 + 0.7 * hash(i + cb.i * 31, 7));
      const d = (120 + 420 * hash(i, 8)) * (1 - slap * 0.6);
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(SELFIE.x - 380 + Math.cos(a) * d, SELFIE.y + 1.5 * SELFIE.r + Math.sin(a) * d, 10 + 16 * hash(i, 9), 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
  },
};

// 10pm and *you're* sleeping: the only rear-camera shot. The camera flips (a squash and a
// blur), then the flash finds a parent asleep in the armchair, head back, mouth open.
import { fillAll, parent, wall } from '../props.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, flashPop, CW, CH } from './common.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { mix } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/theme.js';

export default {
  camera(C) {
    const k = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, C.start, C.start + 0.22));
    return { zoom: 1, shake: 0.8, dx: (1 - k) * 0 };
  },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const { G } = C;
    const k = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, C.start, C.start + 0.22));
    ctx.save();
    // camera-flip squash
    ctx.translate(CW / 2, 0);
    ctx.scale(Math.max(0.02, k), 1);
    ctx.translate(-CW / 2, 0);
    wall(ctx, G, { glow: 0 });
    // the armchair
    const lit = (c) => mix(c, '#ffffff', 0.12);
    ctx.fillStyle = lit(G.seat);
    rrect(ctx, 120, 620, 840, 1200, 140); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = lit(G.seatShade);
    rrect(ctx, 40, 1050, 220, 800, 90); ctx.fill();
    rrect(ctx, 820, 1050, 220, 800, 90); ctx.fill();
    // the parent: head back on the headrest, mouth open, lit by the flash
    parent(ctx, {
      x: 560, y: 700, s: 175, pose: 'sit', color: mix(G.parent, '#8090b0', 0.25), skin: mix('#e8c2a4', '#ffffff', 0.1),
      eyes: 'closed', eyeColor: '#3a2c26', mouth: 'o', lean: -0.18,
    });
    // a blanket over the knees
    ctx.fillStyle = lit(G.sack);
    rrect(ctx, 200, 1500, 760, 500, 120); ctx.fill();
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(560, 900, 200, 560, 900, 1300);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(4,5,12,0)');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(4,5,12,0.8)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
    ctx.restore();
    if (k < 1) { ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#000000', 0.7 * (1 - k)); ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800); }
    flashPop(ctx, C.t - (C.start + 0.22), 0.45);
  },
};

// Final chorus: the day as a filling grid of its clips, 3 wide (a camera-roll grid). Each
// tile lights on its line and plays once, then holds as a thumbnail; the newest tile has a
// white border. The 12th slot is the record button (tomorrow). The 1am tile goes black.
import { ruleFor } from '../clips.js';
import { grade } from '../tod.js';
import { drawCaption } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/caption.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, CW, CH } from './common.js';

export const GRID = { cols: 3, rows: 4, gap: 8, top: 124 };
const TW = (CW - (GRID.cols + 1) * GRID.gap) / GRID.cols;
const TH = (CH - GRID.top - (GRID.rows + 1) * GRID.gap) / GRID.rows;
const TILE_SCALE = TW / CW;
const CROP_H = TH / TILE_SCALE;           // camera-space height a tile shows
const CROP_Y = 1190 - CROP_H * 0.56;      // keep the face in the tile

export function tileRect(i) {
  const c = i % GRID.cols, r = Math.floor(i / GRID.cols);
  return { x: GRID.gap + c * (TW + GRID.gap), y: GRID.top + GRID.gap + r * (TH + GRID.gap), w: TW, h: TH };
}

export default {
  camera() { return { shake: 0, kick: 0.8 }; },
  caption() { return false; },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const { B, env } = C;
    ctx.fillStyle = '#050507';
    ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
    const list = C.params.clauses.map((i) => C.story.clauses[i]);
    const cuts = list.map((c) => B.snap(c.start, 'downbeat'));
    let active = -1;
    cuts.forEach((ct, i) => { if (C.t >= ct - 1e-6) active = i; });
    const endFade = seg(C.t, C.end - B.barDur * 0.5, C.end);
    for (let i = 0; i < GRID.cols * GRID.rows; i++) {
      const R = tileRect(i);
      ctx.save();
      rrect(ctx, R.x, R.y, R.w, R.h, 18);
      ctx.clip();
      if (i < list.length && i <= active) {
        const c = list[i];
        const rule = ruleFor(c);
        const start = cuts[i], dur = (i + 1 < cuts.length ? cuts[i + 1] : C.end) - start;
        // the newest tile plays, older ones hold their thumbnail frame
        const tt = i === active ? C.t : start + dur * 0.8;
        const Ct = {
          ...C, type: rule.type, params: { ...rule.params, clause: c.index }, t: tt, lt: tt - start, start, end: start + dur, dur,
          min: c.min, G: grade(c.min), words: c.words, clause: c, tile: true,
        };
        ctx.translate(R.x, R.y);
        const pop = i === active ? 1 + 0.08 * (1 - ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, start, start + 0.18))) : 1;
        ctx.translate(R.w / 2, R.h / 2); ctx.scale(pop, pop); ctx.translate(-R.w / 2, -R.h / 2);
        ctx.scale(TILE_SCALE, TILE_SCALE);
        ctx.translate(0, -CROP_Y);
        env.drawCameraFrame(ctx, Ct, { chrome: false });
        drawCaption(ctx, tt, c.words, { x: 540, y: CROP_Y + 230, size: 112, minSize: 80, maxW: 1000, family: '"Inter", sans-serif', weight: 900, reveal: i === active ? 'word' : 'all' });
      } else if (i === GRID.cols * GRID.rows - 1) {
        // the 12th slot: the record button, waiting for tomorrow
        ctx.fillStyle = '#111116';
        ctx.fillRect(R.x, R.y, R.w, R.h);
        const pulse = 0.6 + 0.4 * Math.abs(Math.sin(C.t * Math.PI));
        ctx.strokeStyle = rgba('#ffffff', 0.85);
        ctx.lineWidth = 8;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(R.x + R.w / 2, R.y + R.h / 2, 62, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#ff2d2d', pulse);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(R.x + R.w / 2, R.y + R.h / 2, 48, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fill();
      } else {
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0f0f13';
        ctx.fillRect(R.x, R.y, R.w, R.h);
      }
      ctx.restore();
      if (i === active) {
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.lineWidth = 7;
        rrect(ctx, R.x - 2, R.y - 2, R.w + 4, R.h + 4, 20);
        ctx.stroke();
      }
    }
    if (endFade > 0) { ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#000000', 0.85 * endFade); ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800); }
  },
};
EOF

// 10pm and *you're* sleeping: the only rear-camera shot. The camera flips (a squash and a
// dark beat), then the flash finds a parent asleep in the armchair, head back, mouth open.
import { wall } from '../props.js';
import { clamp, ease, seg, rgba, flashPop, CW, CH } from './common.js';
import { rrect } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/text.js';
import { TAU } from '../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';

const K = { chair: '#5f6a7e', chairShade: '#465062', sweater: '#8796ad', sweaterShade: '#6c7a92', skin: '#ecc6a8', skinShade: '#d09f80', hair: '#3a2b24', ink: '#2a1f1a', blanket: '#c9b6a0' };

function sleeper(ctx, t) {
  // armchair
  ctx.fillStyle = K.chair; rrect(ctx, 60, 520, 960, 1500, 180); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = K.chairShade; rrect(ctx, 150, 600, 780, 900, 140); ctx.fill();
  // torso slumped in the chair
  ctx.fillStyle = K.sweater;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(170, 2100); ctx.lineTo(210, 1330); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(250, 1160, 470, 1130);
  ctx.lineTo(650, 1130); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(860, 1160, 890, 1330); ctx.lineTo(930, 2100); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = K.sweaterShade; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(820, 1650, 120, 450, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  // chair arms over the elbows
  ctx.fillStyle = K.chair; rrect(ctx, 0, 1380, 230, 700, 90); ctx.fill(); rrect(ctx, 850, 1380, 230, 700, 90); ctx.fill();
  // neck + head tipped back on the headrest
  ctx.fillStyle = K.skinShade; rrect(ctx, 500, 1030, 120, 140, 40); ctx.fill();
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(560, 900);
  ctx.rotate(-0.22 + 0.01 * Math.sin(t * 1.3));
  ctx.fillStyle = K.skin; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 180, 215, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillStyle = K.hair; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, -95, 192, 140, 0, Math.PI, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.fillRect(-192, -100, 40, 120); ctx.fillRect(152, -100, 40, 120);
  ctx.fillStyle = K.skin; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-182, 20, 30, 50, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(182, 20, 30, 50, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = K.ink; ctx.lineWidth = 9; ctx.lineCap = 'round';
  for (const k of [-1, 1]) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(k * 66, 10, 30, 0.15 * Math.PI, 0.85 * Math.PI); ctx.stroke(); }
  // mouth open, breathing
  const o = 0.8 + 0.2 * Math.sin(t * 2.2);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#5a2626'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 120, 34, 46 * o, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = K.skinShade; ctx.lineWidth = 7; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 55, 16, 0.1 * Math.PI, 0.9 * Math.PI); ctx.stroke();
  ctx.restore();
  // blanket over the lap
  ctx.fillStyle = K.blanket; rrect(ctx, 120, 1700, 840, 600, 140); ctx.fill();
}

export default {
  camera() { return { shake: 0.8 }; },
  draw(ctx, C) {
    const { G } = C;
    const flip = 0.2;
    const k = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, C.start, C.start + flip));
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(CW / 2, 0);
    ctx.scale(Math.max(0.02, k), 1);
    ctx.translate(-CW / 2, 0);
    wall(ctx, G, { glow: 0 });
    sleeper(ctx, C.t);
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(560, 1050, 300, 560, 1050, 1250);
    g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(4,5,12,0)');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(4,5,12,0.82)');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800);
    ctx.restore();
    if (k < 1) { ctx.fillStyle = rgba('#000000', 0.8 * (1 - k)); ctx.fillRect(-900, -900, CW + 1800, CH + 1800); }
    flashPop(ctx, C.t - (C.start + flip), 0.45);
  },
};
EOF

  overlay(ctx, C) {
    if (C.params.prop !== 'point') return;
    // a finger coming straight at the lens: huge, soft, out of focus
    const k = ease.outCubic(seg(C.t, C.start + 0.05, C.start + Math.min(0.5, C.dur * 0.55)));
    const L = babyLight(C, 'onesie');

Writesongs/2am-crying/video/README.md
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/2am-crying/video/README.md
# 2AM (And I'm Crying): the video

Treatment v3, "the baby's selfie video" (`../TREATMENT.md`), over the v3 lyric sheet
(`../suno_lyrics_v3_day.txt`). Every frame is a pure function of song time, painted on a
canvas in headless Chrome and encoded with ffmpeg. The engine is song-agnostic and lives in
`shared/renderer/`; this directory holds the song: clip types, the baby, props, the caption
and status-bar styling, the time-of-day grade and the edit rules.

Review surface: `sheets/plates.png` (one cell per clip, 9:16) and `sheets/layout-16x9.png`.

## Commands

All from the repo root (`/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop`). One-time setup:

```sh
npm install --prefix shared/renderer          # puppeteer-core only; drives the installed Chrome
```

Preview (scrub, play against the audio; space, arrows = beat, shift+arrows = bar, `[` `]` = clip):

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs        # then open:
# http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?t=23
# http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?layout=16x9&t=40
```

Draft render (9:16, 540x960, 30 fps, audio picked from `data/audio.json` "source"):

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video \
  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-540p.mp4 --check
# 16:9 draft: add --layout 16x9 (960x540) and a different --out
```

Final renders (1080p60):

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --w 1080 --h 1920 --fps 60 \
  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1080x1920-60.mp4
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --layout 16x9 --w 1920 --h 1080 --fps 60 \
  --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1920x1080-60.mp4
```

Contact sheets and stills:

```sh
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --sheet songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/plates.png
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --layout 16x9 \
  --sheet songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/layout-16x9.png --sheet-mode even --count 12 --cols 4 --cell-w 480
node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --stills 4.5,40,77
node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --q t=23 --q "layout=16x9&t=40"   # preview page loads cleanly
```

Timing and placeholder audio:

```sh
node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs     # regenerate data/lyrics.json + data/audio.json
node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs --plan   # ignore the synth, use the 128 BPM plan
node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/make_click.mjs      # audio/click.wav (fallback audio, gitignored)
```

`render.mjs` options: `--from/--to` seconds, `--workers N` (default ~60% of cores),
`--crf 16`, `--preset medium`, `--audio file`, `--force` (allow audio/timing length mismatch),
`--check` (determinism: ordered vs shuffled frames across workers; loop: outro frames equal
intro frames). Video is libx264 CRF 16, yuv420p, tagged BT.709; audio AAC 192k.

## Data

- `data/lyrics.json`: `{ lines: [{ i, text, start, end, section, words: [{ w, start, end, conf, syl? }] }], extras, notes }`
  (mexicat/pdoom-video schema; `section` is a convenience, the engine recomputes it from audio.json).
- `data/audio.json`: `{ bpm, beats, downbeats, sections: [{ name, start, end }], kicks, duration }` plus
  added optional fields: `beats_per_bar`, `hats` (offbeat onsets), `quiet` (silent spans: the bar
  after "1am and I'm..."), `risers`, `source` ("plan" or "synth (...)": render.mjs and the preview
  pick the audio file from it), `lyrics_sheet`.

`tools/mock_timing.mjs` builds both. It prefers `../synth/out/song.json` when that arrangement
fits the lyric sheet (same section order, at least a bar per line); it then takes the beat grid,
sections and kicks from the synth and times every word from the synth's sung syllables
(`lyric_lines`, `adlibs`). Otherwise it lays the sheet out on the plan: intro 2, chorus 8,
verse1 8, chorus 8, verse2 9 (8 + the bar of silence after "1am and I'm..."), bridge 8, final
chorus 12, outro 2 = 57 bars at 128 BPM (106.875 s), clauses sharing each line, words spread
evenly over 90% of their span. Current source: **synth** (iteration 7 matches the plan bar for
bar). When the Suno vocal is aligned (`shared/align`), drop its `lyrics.json`/`audio.json` in
`data/`: clips are derived from lyric content, so nothing else changes. Watch the console for
`[clips] ... matched no rule` (a clause whose text drifted falls back to the closeup clip).

Audio: the draft uses `../synth/out/2am_crying.wav` when `audio.json.source` is the synth
(the full-song file, chosen by duration); otherwise `audio/click.wav`, a click + kick + hats +
riser track generated from audio.json by `tools/make_click.mjs` (written in Node, no ffmpeg
filters needed). render.mjs refuses audio whose length differs from audio.json by more than a bar.

## How it is put together

- `clips.js`: splits every lyric line into time clauses ("It's <time> ..."), maps each clause's
  text to a clip type + params (the rule table), and returns the timeline: one window per
  clause (cut on the downbeat; on the half-bar in the double-time verses, so two cuts per bar),
  one long-take window for the bridge, one grid window for the final chorus, and the outro.
- `story.js`: status-bar clock (the clause's time; minute by minute in the long take), battery
  (drains, charges with a bolt during naps, ends the day in the red), record timer, and the
  loop: outro frames are intro frames, quantized to the frame grid (`--check` verifies it).
- `song.js`: the compositor. Clip under the camera transform (handshake + a slight kick shake +
  per-clip tilt, owned here so a clip can be held still) -> lens post (grain on a fixed 24 Hz
  tick, vignette) -> lens overlays (splat, droplets, finger) -> caption -> status bar; then the
  layout: full frame (9:16) or the phone centred on a soft nursery backdrop with a sun/moon
  arc (16:9).
- `tod.js`: time-of-day light (deep blue night, grey-blue 5am, warm 7am, white noon, gold 6pm,
  blue 8pm), the night flash palette, the phone-screen glow.
- `baby.js`, `props.js`, `monitor.js` (the v1 IR monitor view, cut into 7:45 "sleeping").
- `scenes/`: one module per clip type: `hello`, `crib`, `nap`, `pooping`, `closeup`, `highchair`,
  `standing`, `fan`, `bath`, `rear`, `grid`.

## Deliberate departures

- Verse 2 is 9 bars (the brief's 8 plus the bar of silence after "1am and I'm..."), 57 bars total.
- No wide-angle edge stretch: it needs a per-frame offscreen remap for an effect that is barely
  visible at phone size. Day "over-saturation" is carried by the time-of-day grade instead.
- The 12th grid tile (11 final-chorus lines in a 3x4 grid) is the record button rather than a clip.
- The drinking clips hold the bottle with one hand: the other hand is always on the phone.

BashKill hung render; harden browser shutdown12:28 AM
input
description Kill hung render; harden browser shutdown
kill 68106 2>/dev/null; sleep 1; pkill -f "puppeteer_dev_chrome_profile" 2>/dev/null; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs'
s=open(p).read()
old="""  } finally {
    await Promise.all(workers.map((x) => x.browser.close().catch(() => {})));
    server.close();
  }
}"""
new="""  } finally {
    // close each browser, but never hang on it: kill whatever has not exited after 5 s
    await Promise.all(workers.map(async (x) => {
      const proc = x.browser.process();
      await Promise.race([x.browser.close().catch(() => {}), new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 5000))]);
      if (proc && proc.exitCode == null) proc.kill('SIGKILL');
    }));
    server.closeAllConnections?.();
    server.close();
  }
}"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
old="""main().catch((e) => { console.error('[render] FAILED:', e.message || e); process.exit(1); });"""
new="""main().then(() => process.exit(0), (e) => { console.error('[render] FAILED:', e.message || e); process.exit(1); });"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

closeupbottle, book, book corner in the mouth, finger into the lens, fighting it, 5pm crying (no extra witching-hour motif)
fan10:30 whip up to the fan; 5:30 crying at it
bath7pm splashing, droplets on the lens, caption drips
rear10pm: the camera flips to a parent asleep in the armchair
gridfinal chorus as a filling 3-wide grid of the day's clips; the 1am tile goes black

Render time

  • 540p: 5.7 ms/frame wall with 10 workers (about 36 ms per frame in each worker), so the whole song took 18 s. A second run took 47 ms/frame while Spotlight, another agent's Python job and Steam were loading the machine.
  • 1080p60: I timed 4 s of the grid, the heaviest section, at 58 ms/frame wall. A full 1080p60 render should take roughly 3 to 5 minutes; that is an estimate, not a timed run.

Commands (from the repo root; set up once with npm install --prefix shared/renderer)

  • Preview: node shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs, then open http://localhost:8123/songs/2am-crying/video/index.html?t=23 (add &layout=16x9 for the wide layout).
  • Draft: node shared/renderer/scripts/render.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/draft-540p.mp4 --check
  • Final 9:16: the same with --w 1080 --h 1920 --fps 60 --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1080x1920-60.mp4
  • Final 16:9: add --layout 16x9 --w 1920 --h 1080 --fps 60 --out songs/2am-crying/video/renders/final-1920x1080-60.mp4
  • Clip sheet: ... --sheet songs/2am-crying/video/sheets/plates.png. Stills: ... --stills 4.5,40.
  • Timing: node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/mock_timing.mjs (add --plan to ignore the synth).
  • Click track: node songs/2am-crying/video/tools/make_click.mjs
  • Smoke test: node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --q t=23 --q "layout=16x9&t=40"

What I kept from v1 and what I dropped. No v1 plates were finished when the pivot came (there was only a placeholder), so there were no finished scenes to adapt.

  • Kept:
    • the whole engine (timeline, beat utilities, render script, preview), plus a new shared caption component and a layout switch;
    • the old karaoke band, still in the engine but unused by v3;
    • the mock-timing and click tools, rebuilt for v3;
    • the v1 IR monitor look, reused as the 7:45 "sleeping" cut-in.
  • Dropped:
    • the NURSERY CAM HUD;
    • the sleep-debt bar;
    • IR greyscale as the main look;
    • one module per lyric line, replaced by one module per clip type chosen from the lyric text.

Deliberate changes from the brief

  • Verse 2 is 9 bars, not 8: the extra bar is the silence after "1am and I'm...", which makes 57 bars in total.
  • There is no wide-angle edge stretch. Daytime over-saturation is carried by the time-of-day colour grade instead.
  • The 12th grid tile is a record button, because 11 final-chorus lines leave one slot in a 3x4 grid.
  • The drinking clips hold the bottle one-handed, because the other arm is always holding the phone.

Notes

  • Commit attribution: the commits end with Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5, as the system attribution instruction says, rather than the Claude Fable 5.1 line in the task; the user's CLAUDE.md doesn't specify either.
  • Shared repo: other sessions twice swept my uncommitted files into their own commits. Everything of mine is committed, the last commit being 5f970fb, but a few files first landed through their commits.
  • Timing: when the Suno vocal is aligned, its lyrics.json and audio.json drop into data/ with no other changes, because clips are chosen from the lyric text.
